By tightly coupling planning with reward driven execution, our approach yields more coherent and reliable edits than single-step or rule-based multistep baselines.

</details>


### [178] [Warp-as-History: Generalizable Camera-Controlled Video Generation from One Training Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15182)
*Yifan Wang,Tong He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Warp-as-History方法，通过将相机运动引起的变形作为伪历史输入，无需训练或测试时优化即可实现冻结视频生成模型的零样本相机轨迹控制，仅需少量LoRA微调即可进一步提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有相机可控视频生成方法通常需要后训练或测试时优化，作者希望在不引入额外训练和优化成本的前提下，让预训练的视频生成模型具备遵循指定相机轨迹的能力。

Method: 提出Warp-as-History接口：根据目标相机轨迹构造相机变形的伪历史帧，将其通过模型的视觉历史通路输入；对齐伪历史帧与去噪目标帧的位置编码，并移除无效源观测的变形历史token。无需训练、架构修改或测试时优化。可选轻量LoRA微调（仅需一个带相机标注的视频）进一步改进。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验表明，该方法在零样本设置下即可实现相机轨迹跟随，LoRA微调后进一步提升了相机遵从度、视觉质量和运动动态，且能泛化到未见视频。

Conclusion: Warp-as-History为相机可控视频生成提供了一种简洁有效的训练-free方案，证明了冻结的预训练模型具备零样本相机控制能力，且轻量微调可增强该能力。

Abstract: Camera-controlled video generation has made substantial progress, enabling generated videos to follow prescribed viewpoint trajectories. However, existing methods usually learn camera-specific conditioning through camera encoders, control branches, or attention and positional-encoding modifications, which often require post-training on large-scale camera-annotated videos. Training-free alternatives avoid such post-training, but often shift the cost to test-time optimization or extra denoising-time guidance. We propose Warp-as-History, a simple interface that turns camera-induced warps into camera-warped pseudo-history with target-frame positional alignment and visible-token selection. Given a target camera trajectory, we construct camera-warped pseudo-history from past observations and feed it through the model's visual-history pathway. Crucially, we align its positional encoding with the target frames being denoised and remove warped-history tokens without valid source observations. Without any training, architectural modification, or test-time optimization, this interface reveals a non-trivial zero-shot capability of a frozen video generation model to follow camera trajectories. Moreover, lightweight offline LoRA finetuning on only one camera-annotated video further improves this capability and generalizes to unseen videos, improving camera adherence, visual quality, and motion dynamics without test-time optimization or target-video adaptation. Extensive experiments on diverse datasets confirm the effectiveness of our method.

</details>


### [179] [Quantitative Video World Model Evaluation for Geometric-Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15185)
*Jiaxin Wu,Yihao Pi,Yinling Zhang,Yuheng Li,Xueyan Zou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PDI-Bench（透视失真指数），一个用于量化评估生成视频中几何一致性的框架。通过分割、点跟踪将视频提升到3D空间，计算尺度-深度对齐、3D运动一致性、3D结构刚性三个维度的投影几何残差来诊断几何失败。同时构建了PDI-Dataset数据集，揭示现有视频生成器存在常见感知指标无法捕获的几何故障模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成视频模型被越来越多地用作隐式世界模型，但评估其产生的物理合理的3D结构和运动仍然困难。现有视频评估方法严重依赖人类判断或学习型评分器，这些方法主观性强且对几何故障的诊断能力弱。

Method: 对生成的视频片段，通过分割和点跟踪（如SAM 2、MegaSaM、CoTracker3）获取以对象为中心的观察，通过单目重建将其提升到3D世界空间坐标，然后计算一组投影几何残差，涵盖三个失败维度：尺度-深度对齐、3D运动一致性和3D结构刚性。同时构建了PDI-Dataset数据集以支持系统性评估。

Result: 在多个最先进的视频生成器上，PDI揭示了常见的几何特定故障模式，这些模式未被常见的感知指标捕获，并为朝着物理基础视频生成和物理世界模型的进展提供了诊断信号。

Conclusion: PDI-Bench提供了一个可量化的几何审计框架，能够有效诊断生成视频中的几何不一致性，弥补了现有评估指标在几何维度上的不足。

Abstract: Generative video models are increasingly studied as implicit world models, yet evaluating whether they produce physically plausible 3D structure and motion remains challenging. Most existing video evaluation pipelines rely heavily on human judgment or learned graders, which can be subjective and weakly diagnostic for geometric failures. We introduce PDI-Bench (Perspective Distortion Index), a quantitative framework for auditing geometric coherence in generated videos. Given a generated clip, we obtain object-centric observations via segmentation and point tracking (e.g., SAM 2, MegaSaM, and CoTracker3), lift them to 3D world-space coordinates via monocular reconstruction, and compute a set of projective-geometry residuals capturing three failure dimensions: scale-depth alignment, 3D motion consistency, and 3D structural rigidity. To support systematic evaluation, we build PDI-Dataset, covering diverse scenarios designed to stress these geometric constraints. Across state-of-the-art video generators, PDI reveals consistent geometry-specific failure modes that are not captured by common perceptual metrics, and provides a diagnostic signal for progress toward physically grounded video generation and physical world model. Our code and dataset can be found at https://pdi-bench.github.io/.

</details>


### [180] [RAVEN: Real-time Autoregressive Video Extrapolation with Consistency-model GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15190)
*Yanzuo Lu,Ronglai Zuo,Jiankang Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RAVEN训练测试框架和CM-GRPO强化学习方法，解决因果自回归视频扩散模型在长序列生成中因训练与推理历史分布不一致导致的质量退化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果自回归视频扩散模型在训练时遇到的历史分布与推理时实际产生的历史分布存在持续差距，限制了长序列生成质量。现有方法未有效对齐训练注意力与推理时的外推模式。

Method: 1) RAVEN：将每次自回归回滚重新打包成交错序列（干净历史端点 + 噪声去噪状态），使训练注意力与推理外推对齐，并允许下游块损失监督历史表示。2) CM-GRPO：将一致性采样步骤重写为条件高斯转移，直接对核函数应用在线强化学习，避免传统流模型RL中的欧拉-丸山辅助过程。

Result: RAVEN在质量、语义和动态程度评估上均优于近期因果视频蒸馏基线；结合CM-GRPO后性能进一步提升。

Conclusion: 通过训练-测试分布对齐和基于一致性模型的在线强化学习，可显著改善自回归视频扩散模型的长序列生成质量。

Abstract: Causal autoregressive video diffusion models support real-time streaming generation by extrapolating future chunks from previously generated content. Distilling such generators from high-fidelity bidirectional teachers yields competitive few-step models, yet a persistent gap between the history distributions encountered during training and those arising at inference constrains generation quality over long horizons. We introduce the Real-time Autoregressive Video Extrapolation Network (RAVEN), a training-time test framework that repacks each self rollout into an interleaved sequence of clean historical endpoints and noisy denoising states. This formulation aligns training attention with inference-time extrapolation and allows downstream chunk losses to supervise the history representations on which future predictions depend. We further propose Consistency-model Group Relative Policy Optimization (CM-GRPO), which reformulates a consistency sampling step as a conditional Gaussian transition and applies online Reinforcement Learning (RL) directly to this kernel, avoiding the Euler-Maruyama auxiliary process adopted in prior flow-model RL formulations. Experiments demonstrate that RAVEN surpasses recent causal video distillation baselines across quality, semantic, and dynamic degree evaluations, and that CM-GRPO provides further gains when combined with RAVEN.

</details>


### [181] [Aligning Latent Geometry for Spherical Flow Matching in Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15193)
*Tuna Han Salih Meral,Kaan Oktay,Hidir Yesiltepe,Adil Kaan Akan,Pinar Yanardag*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于球面线性插值的潜在流匹配方法，固定潜在半径为常数值，将高斯噪声的径向投影作为球面先验，用球面线性插值替代欧几里得线性插值，使得路径保持在地球面上且速度目标纯角度化，在不改变扩散架构的情况下提升图像生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准潜在流匹配中高斯噪声和VAE潜在都集中在薄球壳上，欧几里得弦会偏离这些球壳，导致路径效率低下；同时发现语义内容主要由角度（方向）承载，径向贡献很小。

Method: 1) 将潜在token分解为径向和角度分量；2) 将数据潜在投影到固定token半径；3) 使用高斯噪声的径向投影作为球面先验；4) 冻结编码器微调解码器；5) 用球面线性插值替换线性插值，使路径始终位于球面上且速度目标纯角度化。

Result: 在类条件ImageNet-256上，该方法一致提升了不同图像分词器的FID指标，且无需辅助编码器或表示对齐目标，扩散架构保持原样。

Conclusion: 通过在潜在空间中引入球面几何约束，可以在不增加模型复杂度的前提下显著改善生成质量，验证了方向信息对语义内容的主导作用。

Abstract: Latent flow matching for image generation usually transports Gaussian noise to variational autoencoder latents along linear paths. Both endpoints, however, concentrate in thin spherical shells, and a Euclidean chord leaves those shells even when preprocessing aligns their radii. By decomposing each latent token into radial and angular components, we show through component-swap probes that decoded perceptual and semantic content is carried predominantly by direction, with radius contributing much less. We therefore project data latents onto a fixed token radius, use the radial projection of Gaussian noise as the spherical prior, finetune the decoder with the encoder frozen, and replace linear interpolation with spherical linear interpolation. The resulting geodesic paths stay on the sphere at every timestep, and their velocity targets are purely angular by construction. Under matched training, the method consistently improves class-conditional ImageNet-256 FID across different image tokenizers, leaves the diffusion architecture unchanged, and requires no auxiliary encoder or representation-alignment objective.

</details>


### [182] [ATLAS: Agentic or Latent Visual Reasoning? One Word is Enough for Both](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15198)
*Ziyu Guo,Rain Liu,Xinyan Chen,Pheng-Ann Heng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ATLAS提出了一种将视觉推理中的智能体操作与潜在推理统一为单一"功能令牌"（functional token）的框架，该令牌既是可执行的操作单元，也是标准词汇表中的离散词元，通过自回归预测生成，无需视觉监督或架构修改。同时提出LA-GRPO方法解决强化学习中功能令牌稀疏性问题，在多个基准上实现了更优性能和清晰的可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉推理方法存在两难：直接生成图像的统一模型计算昂贵且架构复杂；智能体方法（通过代码或工具调用）存在外部执行导致的上下文切换延迟；潜在推理方法（可学习隐嵌入）缺乏任务泛化能力且难以与自回归并行训练兼容。作者希望结合二者优势并克服各自局限。

Method: 提出ATLAS框架，核心是"功能令牌"——单个离散词元同时充当智能体操作和潜在视觉推理单元。每个功能令牌内化了一种视觉操作，但无需视觉监督，作为分词器词汇表中的标准词元，通过标准的下一词元预测生成。同时为解决强化学习中功能令牌稀疏性问题，提出Latent-Anchored GRPO (LA-GRPO)，通过静态加权辅助目标锚定功能令牌以稳定训练并提供更强的梯度更新。

Result: 大量实验和分析表明，ATLAS在多个具有挑战性的基准上取得了优越性能，同时保持了清晰的可解释性。

Conclusion: ATLAS提供了一种新的视觉推理范式，在不修改架构或方法论的前提下，通过功能令牌统一了智能体操作与潜在推理，兼容标准SFT和RL训练，为未来视觉推理研究提供了新思路。

Abstract: Visual reasoning, often interleaved with intermediate visual states, has emerged as a promising direction in the field. A straightforward approach is to directly generate images via unified models during reasoning, but this is computationally expensive and architecturally non-trivial. Recent alternatives include agentic reasoning through code or tool calls, and latent reasoning with learnable hidden embeddings. However, agentic methods incur context-switching latency from external execution, while latent methods lack task generalization and are difficult to train with autoregressive parallelization. To combine their strengths while mitigating their limitations, we propose ATLAS, a framework in which a single discrete 'word', termed as a functional token, serves both as an agentic operation and a latent visual reasoning unit. Each functional token is associated with an internalized visual operation, yet requires no visual supervision and remains a standard token in the tokenizer vocabulary, which can be generated via next-token prediction. This design avoids verbose intermediate visual content generation, while preserving compatibility with the vanilla scalable SFT and RL training, without architectural or methodological modifications. To further address the sparsity of functional tokens during RL, we introduce Latent-Anchored GRPO (LA-GRPO), which stabilizes the training by anchoring functional tokens with a statically weighted auxiliary objective, providing stronger gradient updates. Extensive experiments and analyses demonstrate that ATLAS achieves superior performance on challenging benchmarks while maintaining clear interpretability. We hope ATLAS offers a new paradigm inspiring future visual reasoning research.

</details>


### [183] [EntityBench: Towards Entity-Consistent Long-Range Multi-Shot Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15199)
*Ruozhen He,Meng Wei,Ziyan Yang,Vicente Ordonez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EntityBench是一个包含140个片段（2,491个镜头）的多镜头视频生成基准测试，源自真实叙事媒体，并配有三位一体的评估体系。同时提出EntityMem记忆增强生成系统，实验表明跨镜头实体一致性随重现距离急剧下降，而显式的逐实体记忆方法在角色保真度上表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估通常使用独立生成的提示集，实体覆盖有限且一致性度量简单，难以进行标准化比较。缺乏对跨镜头实体一致性（尤其是长序列中）的系统性评估方法。

Method: 构建EntityBench基准（140个片段/2,491个镜头），采用三位一体评估框架（帧内质量、提示遵循对齐、跨镜头一致性），并设计保真度门控机制。提出EntityMem记忆增强生成系统，在生成前将验证过的逐实体视觉参考存入持久记忆库。

Result: 现有方法中跨镜头实体一致性随重现距离增加而急剧下降；显式的逐实体记忆方法在角色保真度（Cohen's d = +2.33）和出现率上均优于其他方法。

Conclusion: 跨镜头实体一致性是长序列视频生成的关键挑战，显式的记忆增强机制能显著提升角色保真度，EntityBench为标准化评估提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Multi-shot video generation extends single-shot generation to coherent visual narratives, yet maintaining consistent characters, objects, and locations across shots remains a challenge over long sequences. Existing evaluations typically use independently generated prompt sets with limited entity coverage and simple consistency metrics, making standardized comparison difficult. We introduce EntityBench, a benchmark of 140 episodes (2,491 shots) derived from real narrative media, with explicit per-shot entity schedules tracking characters, objects, and locations simultaneously across easy / medium / hard tiers of up to 50 shots, 13 cross-shot characters, 8 cross-shot locations, 22 cross-shot objects, and recurrence gaps spanning up to 48 shots. It is paired with a three-pillar evaluation suite that disentangles intra-shot quality, prompt-following alignment, and cross-shot consistency, with a fidelity gate that admits only accurate entity appearances into cross-shot scoring. As a baseline, we propose EntityMem, a memory-augmented generation system that stores verified per-entity visual references in a persistent memory bank before generation begins. Experiments show that cross-shot entity consistency degrades sharply with recurrence distance in existing methods, and that explicit per-entity memory yields the highest character fidelity (Cohen's d = +2.33) and presence among methods evaluated. Code and data are available at https://github.com/Catherine-R-He/EntityBench/.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [184] [AgentTrap: Measuring Runtime Trust Failures in Third-Party Agent Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13940)
*Haomin Zhuang,Hanwen Xing,Yujun Zhou,Yuchen Ma,Yue Huang,Yili Shen,Yufei Han,Xiangliang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: AgentTrap是一个动态基准测试框架，用于评估LLM智能体在使用第三方技能时抵抗恶意运行时行为的能力，包含141个任务（91个恶意+50个良性），覆盖16个安全维度。核心发现是：最有信息价值的失败并非简单的越狱攻击，而是模型在完成可见用户任务的同时，将技能引入的不安全副作用视为正常流程的一部分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第三方技能已成为LLM智能体的包生态系统，但它们引入了新的安全问题：恶意技能可以将有害行为伪装成常规工作流的一部分，利用智能体的高价值权限和有限人工监督来执行恶意行为。现有研究缺乏对此类威胁的系统性评估。

Method: 构建了包含141个任务的动态基准测试，其中91个恶意任务和50个良性任务，覆盖16个安全维度（基于智能体技能供应链威胁）。每个任务中，智能体接收普通用户请求，运行可能包含恶意工作流元素的已安装技能，在沙箱环境中执行。通过完整轨迹判断攻击成功、阻止/拒绝、攻击未触发和无攻击证据四种结果。

Result: 核心发现是：最具信息价值的失败模式并非简单的越狱攻击，而是模型在完成可见用户任务的过程中，将技能引入的不安全副作用视为正常流程的一部分，从而被动执行恶意行为。

Conclusion: 需要在用户实际委托工作的具体模型-框架-工作区环境中进行运行时评估，以有效检测和防御此类隐蔽的供应链攻击。

Abstract: Third-party skills are becoming the package ecosystem for LLM agents. They package natural-language instructions, helper scripts, templates, documents, and service configuration into reusable workflows. This makes skills useful, but it also introduces a new security problem: a malicious skill does not need to ask the model to perform an obviously harmful action. Instead, it can disguise the harmful behavior as part of a routine workflow, relying on the agent to execute that workflow with high-value permissions and limited human supervision.
  We introduce AgentTrap, a dynamic benchmark for evaluating whether LLM agents can use third-party skills while resisting malicious runtime behavior. AgentTrap contains 141 tasks: 91 malicious tasks and 50 benign utility tasks, covering 16 security-impact dimensions grounded in agent-skill supply-chain threats. In each task, the agent receives an ordinary user request, runs with installed skills that may contain malicious workflow elements, and is executed in a sandboxed environment. AgentTrap then judges complete trajectories for attack success, blocked or refused behavior, attack-not-triggered cases, and no-attack-evidence outcomes. Our central finding is that the most informative failures are not simple jailbreaks. Models often complete the visible user task while treating unsafe side effects introduced by the skill as part of the normal workflow. This motivates runtime evaluation of the concrete model--framework--workspace environment in which users actually delegate work. Code and data are available at https://github.com/zhmzm/AgentTrap and https://huggingface.co/datasets/zhmzm/AgentTrap.

</details>


### [185] [Memory Forensics Techniques for Automated Detection and Analysis of Go Malware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14020)
*Hala Ali,Andrew Case,Irfan Ahmed*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个针对Go二进制程序运行时分析的記憶體取證框架，通过解析Go内部结构、感知ABI的向后分析以及goroutine栈分析，能够恢复运行时执行状态和内存驻留的工件（如类型元数据、字符串、函数参数、执行路径等），并在BRICKSTORM后门、Obscura勒索软件等真实恶意软件中成功恢复C2端点、加密密钥、赎金票据等关键信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有静态分析和逆向工程工具对Go二进制程序的分析局限于编译时产物，无法恢复仅存在于内存中的运行时执行状态和工件，导致对Go恶意软件的分析存在盲区。

Method: 1) 解析Go内部结构以重建类型和函数元数据；2) 恢复堆分配和静态字符串；3) 区分应用层函数；4) 通过ABI感知的向后分析从调用点推导执行路径和参数值；5) 分析goroutine栈以识别正在执行的函数并恢复运行时参数值；6) 所有功能实现为Volatility 3插件。

Result: 在BRICKSTORM后门、Obscura勒索软件、Pantegana RAT等真实恶意软件及开源样本上评估，成功恢复了C2端点、持久化机制、加密密钥、赎金票据和执行状态，包括未出现在公开威胁情报中的关键运行时工件。

Conclusion: 该框架填补了Go二进制程序运行时内存取证的空白，能够捕获静态分析无法获取的运行时状态，对Go恶意软件的分析和取证具有重要价值。

Abstract: The Go programming language has become increasingly popular among malware developers due to its ability to produce statically linked, cross-platform executables that challenge traditional analysis techniques. These binaries embed a substantial runtime and compiler-generated metadata and are compiled with aggressive optimizations that discard type information for function parameters and local variables. Go's design further complicates analysis by representing strings as pointer-length pairs rather than null-terminated sequences, employing a caller-allocated stack model that obscures argument boundaries, and fragmenting program state across concurrent goroutines. Although existing static analysis and reverse engineering tools provide Go-specific support, they remain limited to compile-time artifacts and cannot recover runtime execution state and artifacts that persist solely in memory. To address this gap, we present the first memory forensics framework for runtime analysis of Go binaries. By parsing Go's internal structures, our framework reconstructs type and function metadata, recovers heap-allocated and static strings, and distinguishes application-level functions. Through ABI-aware backward analysis, it derives execution paths and argument values from call sites. To capture runtime state beyond what static analysis reveals, it analyzes goroutine stacks to identify actively executing functions and recover their runtime argument values. We implemented all capabilities as Volatility 3 plugins and evaluated them against malware seen in recent incidents, such as the BRICKSTORM backdoor, Obscura ransomware, and Pantegana RAT, as well as open-source samples for reproducibility. The framework successfully recovered C2 endpoints, persistence mechanisms, encryption keys, ransom notes, and execution state, including critical runtime artifacts that were absent from published threat intelligence.

</details>


### [186] [ExploitBench: A Capability Ladder Benchmark for LLM Cybersecurity Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14153)
*Seunghyun Lee,David Brumley*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ExploitBench 是一个能力分级的漏洞利用基准测试，将利用过程分解为16个可测量的能力标志，并通过确定性预言机验证。在41个V8漏洞上测试发现，公开模型能实现崩溃触发但无法实现任意代码执行，而私有模型在约半数案例中实现了任意代码执行，表明针对加固目标的漏洞利用构建是一项新兴的前沿能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM安全基准将崩溃视为利用成功，是二元的单一结果，忽略了从触发漏洞到构建可重用原语和控制权转移的"困难部分"——即渐进式能力获取的阶梯。

Method: 提出ExploitBench，将利用过程分解为16个可测量能力标志（覆盖、崩溃→沙箱原语→任意读写→控制流劫持→任意代码执行），使用每轮随机挑战-响应原语、基于ground-truth二进制的差异执行和信号处理程序证明三种确定性预言机进行验证。在41个V8漏洞上设置三组实验：<模型,环境>主测量、<模型,环境,自适应指导>次测量、<模型,环境,工具集>消融实验。

Result: 8个公开部署的前沿模型能常规触发漏洞和崩溃，但无法实现任意代码执行；私有模型在约半数案例中实现了任意代码执行。

Conclusion: 针对加固目标（如V8）的漏洞利用构建是LLM的一项新兴前沿能力，公开模型与私有模型之间存在显著能力差距。

Abstract: Exploitation is not a binary event. It is a ladder of acquiring progressive capabilities, from executing a single buggy line of code to taking full control of the target. However, existing LLM security benchmarks treat a crash as exploitation success. That single binary outcome collapses the hard parts of exploitation: the transition from triggering a bug to constructing reusable primitives and control.
  We present ExploitBench, a capability-graded benchmark that decomposes exploitation into 16 measurable flags, from coverage and crash through sandbox primitives, arbitrary read/write, control-flow hijack, and arbitrary code execution. Each capability is verified by a deterministic oracle that uses a per-run randomized challenge-response for primitives, differential execution against ground-truth binaries to measure progress, and a signal-handler proof for code execution.
  We instantiate ExploitBench on 41 V8 bugs because V8 is both widely deployed and exploitation-hardened. We report three arms: <model,env> as the primary measurement of model-environment capability, <model,env, adaptive coaching> as a secondary arm that adds adaptive coaching to test whether targeted feedback shifts outcomes, and <model,env,harness> as an ablation that swaps in the model's native CLI to check whether vendor-side optimizations increase exploitation capabilities.
  Our results show a sharp capability split between publicly deployed frontier models and the private frontier. Across the 8 publicly deployed models tested, reaching the vulnerable code and triggering a crash is routine, but arbitrary code execution is not. The private model shows arbitrary code execution on approximately half. Overall, results suggest that exploit construction against hardened targets is an emerging frontier capability.

</details>


### [187] [DSTAN-Med: Dual-Channel Spatiotemporal Attention with Physiological Plausibility Filtering for False Data Injection Attack Detection in IoT-Based Medical Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14165)
*Md Mehedi Hasan,Rafiqul Islam,Md Zakir Hossain*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出DSTAN-Med框架，通过双通道注意力机制（分离传感器维度和时间维度的自注意力）、残差1D-CNN和生理合理性过滤器（PPF）检测IoMT传感器流中的虚假数据注入攻击，在三个数据集上比最强Transformer基线（TranAD）灵敏度提升7.4-8.3个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习检测器将传感器间空间相关性与时间依赖性混淆在同一隐空间中，无法解耦FDI攻击同时留下的独特空间和时间特征；也没有方法利用领域知识约束输出，排除生理上不可能的攻击模式。

Method: 提出DSTAN-Med监督框架，包含：(1) 双通道注意力机制(DAM)，通过独立的传感器维度自注意力(SWA)和时间维度自注意力(TWA)在正交张量轴上处理多变量传感器窗口；(2) 残差1D-CNN模块提取局部时间特征；(3) 零参数生理合理性过滤器(PPF)，抑制违反领域知识边界的攻击特征。

Result: 在PhysioNet/CinC 2012、MIMIC-III Waveform和WESAD三个数据集上，DSTAN-Med比最强Transformer基线TranAD灵敏度提升7.4-8.3个百分点（p<0.01，McNemar检验，Holm-Bonferroni校正）。PPF独立贡献3.1-4.2个百分点的精确率提升，灵敏度损失可忽略。消融实验证实每个组件均不可或缺，移除残差连接导致灵敏度下降14.0个百分点。

Conclusion: DSTAN-Med通过解耦空间和时间注意力路径并引入生理合理性约束，有效检测IoMT传感器流中的FDI攻击，显著超越现有方法。

Abstract: False data injection (FDI) attacks on Internet of Medical Things (IoMT) sensor streams falsify vital signs in transit, threatening patient safety and defeating clinical monitoring systems that lack cyber-physical anomaly detection capability. Existing deep learning detectors conflate inter-sensor spatial correlations with temporal dependencies in a shared latent space, preventing disentanglement of the distinct spatial and temporal signatures that FDI attacks imprint simultaneously; no current method exploits domain knowledge to constrain outputs against physiologically impossible attack patterns. We propose DSTAN-Med, a supervised framework comprising a Dual-channel Attention Mechanism (DAM) that routes multivariate sensor windows through independent sensor-wise (SWA) and time-wise (TWA) self-attention pathways operating on orthogonal tensor axes, a residual 1D-CNN block for local temporal feature extraction, and a zero-parameter Physiological Plausibility Filter (PPF) that suppresses attack signatures violating domain-knowledge bounds. Evaluated across three IoMT sensor datasets - PhysioNet/CinC 2012 (ICU vital signs), MIMIC-III Waveform (continuous ICU waveforms), and WESAD (wearable biosensor signals) - DSTAN-Med achieves mean sensitivity gains of 7.4-8.3 percentage points over the strongest Transformer baseline (TranAD), with improvements significant at p < 0.01 (McNemar's test, Holm-Bonferroni correction). The PPF contributes independent precision gains of 3.1-4.2 percentage points at negligible sensitivity cost across all three corpora. Ablation studies confirm that each component is individually necessary; removal of residual connections alone reduces sensitivity by 14.0 percentage points. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/mehedi93hasan/DSTAN-MED.

</details>


### [188] [Web Agents Should Adopt the Plan-Then-Execute Paradigm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14290)
*Julien Piet,Annabella Chow,Yiwei Hou,Muxi Lyu,Sylvie Venuto,Jinhao Zhu,Raluca Ada Popa,David Wagner*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文批判了ReAct架构在Web智能体中的主流地位，提出应默认采用"先规划后执行"（plan-then-execute）范式，以避免不受信任的网页内容通过提示注入操纵智能体控制流。研究表明WebArena所有任务均兼容该范式，80%可纯编程完成，但关键在于需要类型化、语义化的Web API接口而非当前脆弱的点击/输入工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ReAct架构下，网页内容（如商品页面含卖家信息、评论、广告等多方输入）直接流入模型决策过程，为提示注入提供了直接路径，攻击者可借此劫持智能体的控制流。为此需要一种更安全的默认架构。

Method: 提出plan-then-execute范式：在观察运行时网页内容之前，先承诺一个任务特定的程序，然后执行它。未信任数据只能影响预定义执行图内的值或分支，但不能重新定义用户任务或导致模型在运行时合成新动作。通过分析WebArena基准测试验证可行性。

Result: WebArena所有任务与plan-then-execute兼容，80%可仅用纯编程计划完成，无需运行时LLM子程序。当前浏览器工具（点击、输入、滚动）具有页面依赖的语义，在此层面规划是短视的，需要将网站交互从点击/按键提升到任务级操作的类型化接口。

Conclusion: Web任务默认不需要反应性，而是需要类型化、完整、可审计的网站API。这是一个基础设施问题，而非建模问题。

Abstract: ReAct has become the default architecture across LLM agents, and many existing web agents follow this paradigm. We argue that it is the wrong default for web agents. Instead, web agents should default to plan-then-execute: commit to a task-specific program before observing runtime web content, then execute it. The reason is that web content mixes inputs from many parties. An e-commerce product page may combine a seller's listing, customer reviews and sponsored advertisements. Under ReAct, all of this content flows into the model when deciding on the next action, creating a direct path for prompt injections to steer the agent's control flow. Plan-then-execute changes this boundary: untrusted data may influence values or branches inside a predefined execution graph, but it cannot redefine the user task or cause the model to synthesize new actions at runtime. We analyze WebArena, a popular web agent benchmark, and find that all tasks are compatible with plan-then-execute, while 80% can be completed with a purely programmatic plan, without any runtime LLM subroutine. We identify the main barrier to adopting plan-then-execute on the web: For it to work well, tools must map cleanly to semantic actions, with effects known before execution, so agents have enough information to plan. The web does not naturally expose that interface. Browser tools such as click, type, and scroll have page-dependent meanings. Planning at this layer is near-sighted: the agent can only see actions on the current page, and later actions appear only after it acts. Closing this gap requires typed interfaces that turn website interactions from clicks and keystrokes to task-level operations. This is an infrastructure problem, not a modeling problem. Web tasks do not need reactivity by default; they need typed, complete, auditable website APIs.

</details>


### [189] [To See is Not to Learn: Protecting Multimodal Data from Unauthorized Fine-Tuning of Large Vision-Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14291)
*Chengshuai Zhao,Zhen Tan,Dawei Li,Zhiyuan Yu,Huan Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MMGuard提出一种针对大型视觉语言模型(LVLM)的主动防御方法，通过在多模态数据中注入人眼不可察觉的扰动生成不可学习样本，使模型在微调时过拟合噪声，从而在推理阶段性能下降。该方法结合跨模态绑定干扰和集成学习策略，在9个开源LVLM和6个数据集上验证了白盒、灰盒和黑盒场景下的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有版权保护方法（如机器遗忘、水印）均为事后补救措施，只能在知识产权侵权发生后起作用。亟需一种主动防御手段，在数据被未经授权的LVLM微调之前就实现保护。

Method: 1) 注入人眼不可察觉的扰动生成不可学习样本，通过最小化训练损失创建优化捷径，使模型过拟合噪声；2) 跨模态绑定干扰机制，策略性地偏移LVLM注意力，强制噪声与训练目标之间产生虚假相关性（有理论保证）；3) 集成学习策略提升跨模型迁移性。

Result: 在9个开源LVLM和6个数据集上的综合实验表明，MMGuard在白盒、灰盒和黑盒威胁模型下均能提供有效、隐蔽且鲁棒的保护。

Conclusion: MMGuard建立了对抗激进微调攻击的机械优势，是一种主动防御策略，能够在侵权发生前保护多模态数据。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) is increasingly accompanied by unauthorized scraping and training on multimodal web data, posing severe copyright and privacy risks to data owners. Existing countermeasures, such as machine unlearning and watermarks, are inherent post-hoc approaches that act only after intellectual property infringement has already occurred. In this work, we propose MMGuard to empower data owners to proactively protect their multimodal data against unauthorized LVLM fine-tuning. MMGuard generates unlearnable examples by injecting human-imperceptible perturbations that actively exploit the learning dynamics of LVLMs. By minimizing the training loss, the perturbation creates an optimization shortcut, causing the model to overfit to the noise and thereby degrading downstream performance when the perturbation is absent during inference. To further strengthen this defense, MMGuard introduces a cross-modal binding disruption, strategically shifting LVLM attention to enforce a spurious correlation between the noise and the training target with theoretical guarantees. Enhanced by an ensemble learning strategy for cross-model transferability, MMGuard is evaluated against nine open-source LVLMs across six datasets. Our comprehensive results demonstrate effective, stealthy, and robust protection under white-box, gray-box, and black-box threat models, establishing a mechanistic advantage in proactively defending against aggressive fine-tuning exploitation.

</details>


### [190] [The Great Pretender: A Stochasticity Problem in LLM Jailbreak](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14418)
*Jean-Philippe Monteuuis,Cong Chen,Jonathan Petit*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了LLM越狱攻击评估中的关键问题：攻击成功率（ASR）因随机性而不稳定，导致已发表的ASR数值被系统性夸大且无法跨论文比较。作者提出了两个新框架——CAS-eval（评估框架）和CAS-gen（攻击生成框架），并引入新指标。实验表明，当越狱提示需要在多次尝试中持续成功时，ASR可下降高达30个百分点；而CAS-gen能帮助现有越狱方法恢复这一损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观察到已发表的越狱攻击ASR存在不一致性：一个在论文中声称ASR达80%的攻击方法，在实际重复测试中仅50%连续成功率（5/10次），说明ASR不是一个稳定的度量指标；已发表的ASR数字被系统性夸大且不可跨论文比较。核心研究问题：为什么一个成功的越狱提示在针对其优化过的目标模型上无法表现一致？

Method: 研究随机性在攻击评估和攻击生成两个阶段的影响；评估涵盖多种越狱攻击、不同规模与供应商的模型以及多种评判器；提出新指标；设计CAS-eval评估框架和CAS-gen攻击生成框架。

Result: CAS-eval框架显示，当要求越狱提示在多次尝试中持续成功时，ASR可下降高达30个百分点；CAS-gen框架能帮助现有越狱方法恢复这30个百分点的损失。

Conclusion: 越狱攻击评估中的随机性严重影响ASR的可靠性，需要引入更稳定的评估方法；CAS-eval和CAS-gen分别从评估和生成两个角度解决了这一问题，为越狱攻击基准测试提供了更可靠的方案。

Abstract: "Oh-Oh, yes, I'm the great pretender. Pretending that I'm doing well. My need is such, I pretend too much..." summarizes the state in the area of jailbreak creation and evaluation. You find this method to generate adversarial attacks proposed by a reputable institution (e.g., BoN from Anthropic or Crescendo from Microsoft Research). However, this method does not deliver on the promise claimed in the paper despite having top ASR scores against industry-grade LLMs. You successfully generate the jailbreak prompts against your target (open) model. However, the generated jailbreak prompt works against the target model with a 50% consecutive success rate (5 out of 10 attempts) despite having an 80% ASR (on paper) on the latest closed-source model (with a guardrail system)! This observation leads us to think. First, Attack Success Rate (ASR), the primary metric for LLM jailbreak benchmarking, is not a stable quantity. Second, published ASR numbers are therefore systematically inflated and incomparable across papers. Therefore, we wonder "Why a successful jailbreak prompt does not perform consistently well against a target model on which the prompts have been optimized?". To answer this question, we study the impact of stochasticity not only during attack evaluation but also during attack generation. Our evaluation includes several jailbreak attacks, models (different sizes and providers), and judges. In addition, we propose a new metric and two new frameworks (CAS-eval and CAS-gen). Our evaluation framework, CAS-eval, shows that an attack can have an ASR drop of up to 30 percentage points when a jailbreak prompt needs to succeed on more than one attempt. Thankfully, our attack generation framework (CAS-gen) improves previous jailbreak methods and helps them recover this loss of 30 percentage points!

</details>


### [191] [MemLineage: Lineage-Guided Enforcement for LLM Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14421)
*Ciyan Ouyang,Rui Hou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MemLineage 是一种针对 LLM 智能体记忆的防御机制，通过密码学溯源和 LLM 中介的衍生谱系将每个记忆条目组织成 Merkle 日志和加权派生 DAG，采用最大强边传播规则和敏感行为门控，在消除恶意记忆注入攻击的同时保持正常召回功能，经测试可将攻击成功率降至零，且开销远低于 LLM 调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究发现不可信内容可被写入持久化智能体状态，并在后续会话中作为指令重新进入，现有工作未解决如何在保留有用记忆召回的同时防止此类状态证明敏感行为的系统性问题。

Method: 将记忆安全视为链上保管（chain-of-custody）问题而非过滤问题。设计六模块系统：基于 RFC-6962 Merkle 日志，每个主体使用 Ed25519 签名条目；构建加权派生 DAG 记录检索条目对新记忆的影响；采用最大强边传播规则实现不可信路径持久性；敏感行为门控拒绝源自外部祖先的活跃证明分派。

Result: 在确定性机制隔离测试中，MemLineage 是唯一将三种记忆投毒负载的攻击成功率降至零的配置；每操作亚毫秒开销远低于 LLM 调用噪声底限。在 Codex 驱动的 AgentDojo 桥接测试中，所有 MemLineage 配置将 AgentDojo 的 ASR 降至零，而无防御和仅签名基线全部失败。

Conclusion: MemLineage 通过密码学溯源与谱系追踪有效解决了 LLM 智能体记忆中的恶意注入问题，在不牺牲正常记忆召回的前提下实现了零攻击成功率，且性能开销可忽略不计。

Abstract: We introduce MemLineage, a defense for LLM agent memory that attaches both cryptographic provenance and LLM-mediated derivation lineage to every entry. Recent and concurrent work shows that untrusted content can be written into persistent agent state and re-enter later sessions as an instruction; the remaining systems question is how to preserve useful memory recall while preventing such state from justifying sensitive actions. MemLineage treats this as a chain-of-custody problem rather than a filtering problem. It is a six-module design around an RFC-6962 Merkle log over per-principal Ed25519-signed entries: a weighted derivation DAG records which retrieved entries influenced each new memory, and a max-of-strong-edges propagation rule makes Untrusted-Path Persistence hold for any chain whose attribution edges remain above threshold. The sensitive-action gate then refuses dispatches whose active justification descends from an external ancestor, while still allowing benign recall. We evaluate three defense cells against three memory-poisoning workloads on a deterministic mechanism-isolation harness; MemLineage is the only configuration in that harness that drives all three columns to zero ASR, while sub-millisecond per-operation overhead keeps it well below the noise floor of any LLM call. A Codex-backed AgentDojo bridge further separates strong-model behavior from defense-layer behavior: under an intentionally vulnerable tool-output profile, no-defense and signature-only baselines fail on all six banking pairs, while all MemLineage rows reduce strict AgentDojo ASR to zero. The core deterministic artifacts are byte-equal CI-verified; hosted-model AgentDojo and live-model sweeps are recorded as auditable logs rather than byte-pinned artifacts.

</details>


### [192] [Defenses at Odds: Measuring and Explaining Defense Conflicts in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14514)
*Xiangtao Meng,Wenyu Chen,Chuanchao Zang,Xinyu Gao,Jianing Wang,Li Wang,Zheng Li,Shanqing Guo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 首次系统研究顺序部署下LLM防御间的交叉影响，发现38.9%的序列存在风险加剧，通过机制分析揭示冲突源于关键层的反对齐参数更新，并提出冲突引导的层冻结缓解方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM防御几乎都在一次性部署假设下孤立评估，但实际中模型会经历增量修补的生命周期，这引发了一个根本性问题：后续防御能否保留先前建立的保护？

Method: 评估144个有序序列在三个风险维度和三个模型家族上的表现；使用逐层表征散度和激活修补进行机制分析；引入层间冲突分数量化几何张力；提出冲突引导的层冻结方法。

Result: 38.9%的序列在原始防御维度上出现可测量的风险加剧；冲突具有高度不对称性和顺序依赖性；冲突序列中关键层出现强烈反对齐参数更新，而良性顺序保持近正交更新；PCA轨迹分析揭示防御崩溃源于共享层中的激活模式反转。

Conclusion: 顺序部署下防御间存在显著相互干扰，冲突引导的层冻结能有效缓解该问题，在不降低次要防御性能的前提下保留先前保护。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) deployed in high-stakes applications must simultaneously manage multiple risks, yet existing defenses are almost exclusively evaluated in isolation under a one-shot deployment assumption. In practice, providers patch models incrementally throughout their lifecycle-responding to newly exposed vulnerabilities or targeted data-removal requests without retraining from scratch. This raises a fundamental but underexplored question: does a later defense preserve the protections established by an earlier one? We present the first systematic study of cross-defense interactions under sequential deployment. Evaluating 144 ordered sequences across three risk dimensions and three model families, we find that 38.9% exhibit measurable risk exacerbation on the originally defended dimension. These interactions are highly asymmetric and order-dependent. To explain these phenomena, we conduct a mechanistic analysis on representative deployment sequences. Using layer-wise representational divergence and activation patching, we localize each defense to a compact set of critical layers. In conflicting sequences, the overlapping critical layers exhibit strongly anti-aligned parameter updates, whereas benign orderings maintain near-orthogonal updates. PCA trajectory analysis reveals that defense collapse stems from activation pattern reversals in these shared layers. We further introduce a layer-wise conflict score that quantifies the geometric tension between defense-induced activation subspaces, offering mechanistic insight into the observed reversals. Guided by this diagnosis, we propose conflict-guided layer freezing, a lightweight mitigation that selectively freezes high-conflict layers during sequential deployment, preserving prior protections without degrading secondary defense performance.

</details>


### [193] [Privacy Auditing with Zero (0) Training Run](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14591)
*Tudor Cebere,Mathieu Even,Linus Bleistein,Aurélien Bellet*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出Zero-Run隐私审计框架，在无需重训练或随机化数据包含的情况下，利用固定成员和非成员数据集对模型进行事后差分隐私审计，并通过因果推断方法校正分布偏移带来的混淆效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐私审计方法需要对训练流程进行干预（如多次重训练或随机化数据包含），这对于大型已部署系统（如基础模型）往往不可行，因此需要一种仅使用固定数据集的事后审计方法。

Method: 提出Zero-Run审计框架，包含两种互补校正方法：(1) 全局校正：将分布偏移与算法泄露的联合效应建模为自适应组合，产生保守的全局下界；(2) 实例级校正：基于观测数据条件化，逐点调整成员猜测，得到更尖锐的实例相关下界。

Result: 在合成数据和大规模模型上的实验表明，Zero-Run审计在无法重训练或控制数据插入的情况下实现了实用的隐私评估。

Conclusion: Zero-Run框架为不可干预训练流程的大型模型提供了一种可行的事后差分隐私审计方案，通过因果推断思想有效处理了分布混淆问题。

Abstract: Privacy auditing provides empirical lower bounds on the differential privacy parameters of learning algorithms. Existing methods, however, require interventional access to the training pipeline, either to retrain multiple times or to randomize data inclusion. This is often infeasible for large deployed systems such as foundation models. We introduce Zero-Run privacy auditing, a post-hoc framework for auditing models using two fixed datasets: examples known to be training-set members and examples known to be non-members. In this observational regime, membership is no longer randomized; instead, member and non-member data often differ in distribution, so membership inference scores may reflect a distribution shift rather than algorithmic leakage. Drawing on ideas from causal inference, we formalize this confounding effect and propose two complementary corrections that yield valid privacy audits. Our first approach models the combined effect of distribution shift and algorithmic leakage as an adaptive composition, producing conservative global corrections. Our second approach conditions on observed data and adjusts pointwise membership guesses, yielding sharper instance-dependent bounds. Experiments on synthetic data and large-scale models show that Zero-Run auditing enables practical privacy evaluation when retraining or controlled data insertion is infeasible.

</details>


### [194] [One Step to the Side: Why Defenses Against Malicious Finetuning Fail Under Adaptive Adversaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14605)
*Itay Zloczower,Eyal Lenga,Gilad Gressel,Yisroel Mirsky*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了15种针对大模型微调安全的防御方法，发现它们都未能从根本上消除有害行为，而是通过模糊或误导路径来防御。作者提出了统一的适应性攻击方法，成功攻破了所有防御机制，表明当前防御只对特定攻击有效，无法提供鲁棒的安全保障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御方法仅在固定攻击场景下评估，未考虑攻击者针对防御进行自适应调整的情况，导致对模型鲁棒性的声称不完整。

Method: 识别15种近期防御方法背后的防御机制，发现其共同弱点：仅隐藏或误导有害行为路径而非消除行为本身。开发统一的适应性攻击方法，针对不同防御机制进行攻击。

Result: 统一的适应性攻击成功突破了所有防御机制，证明当前防御方法无法提供鲁棒的安全保障，仅能阻止它们设计时所针对的攻击。

Conclusion: 现有微调防御方法不具备鲁棒安全性，希望提出的统一自适应攻击方法能帮助研究者和实践者在部署前对防御进行压力测试。

Abstract: Model providers increasingly release open weights or allow users to fine-tune foundation models through APIs. Although these models are safety-aligned before release, their safeguards can often be removed by fine-tuning on harmful data. Recent defenses aim to make models robust to such malicious fine-tuning, but they are largely evaluated only against fixed attacks that do not account for the defense. We show that these robustness claims are incomplete. Surveying 15 recent defenses, we identify several defense mechanisms and show that they share a single weakness: they obscure or misdirect the path to harmful behavior without removing the behavior itself. We then develop a unified adaptive attack that breaks defenses across all defense mechanisms. Our results show that current approaches do not provide robust security; they mainly stop the attacks they were designed against. We hope that our unified adaptive adversary for this domain will help future researchers and practitioners stress-test new defenses before deployment.

</details>


### [195] [Capacitive Touchscreens at Risk: A Practical Side-Channel Attack on Smartphones via Electromagnetic Emanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14633)
*Yukun Cheng,Changhai Ou,Shiyu Zhu,Jinyuan Zhang,Zhenfang Qiu,Xingshuo Han,Tianwei Zhang,Yuan Li,Shihui Zheng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出TESLA，一种利用电容触摸屏扫描时电磁辐射进行无接触侧信道攻击的新方法，能高精度窃取PIN码、键盘输入、应用类别和手写轨迹等敏感信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有触摸屏侧信道攻击往往需要严格条件或侵入式测量，缺乏一种在真实场景下可行、无接触且能广泛窃取多种敏感信息的攻击方法。

Method: 将电磁探头秘密放置在受害者设备附近，捕获触摸屏扫描过程中固有的电磁辐射信号，利用这些信号编码的时空演化信息来推断触摸交互内容。

Result: 在iPhone X、小米10 Pro、三星S10和华为Mate 30 Pro上测试，PIN码识别成功率99.3%，键盘输入重建97.6%，应用推断95.0%，字符识别准确率76.8%，2D手写轨迹重建Jaccard指数0.74。

Conclusion: TESLA证明了电磁辐射侧信道攻击在真实场景下的可行性和严重威胁，攻击目标范围更广、样本获取更高效，对智能手机安全构成重大挑战。

Abstract: Capacitive touchscreens in modern smartphones introduce severe side-channel vulnerabilities. However, existing attacks often require restrictive conditions or invasive measurements. This paper presents TESLA, a novel, contactless electromagnetic (EM) side-channel attack that exploits inherent EM emanations during touchscreen scanning. We demonstrate that these emanations encode the spatiotemporal evolution of touch interactions, forming a unified leakage basis. By secretly placing an EM probe near the victim's device, TESLA enables attackers to extract highly sensitive information, including screen-unlocking PIN codes, keyboard inputs, interacting application categories, and continuous handwriting trajectories. Compared to existing attacks, TESLA offers a broader range of attack targets, more efficient sample acquisition, and operations in practical attack scenarios. Extensive evaluations on popular commercial smartphones, specifically the iPhone X, Xiaomi 10 Pro, Samsung S10, and Huawei Mate 30 Pro, validate the effectiveness of TESLA. It achieves remarkable inference accuracy in diverse settings such as private meeting rooms and public libraries, with success rates of 99.3% for PIN code recognition, 97.6% for keyboard input reconstruction, and 95.0% for application inference, respectively. Simultaneously, it attains a 76.8% character recognition accuracy and a high geometric similarity (Jaccard index of 0.74) for 2D handwriting trajectory reconstruction.

</details>


### [196] [Adapting AlphaEvolve to Optimize Fully Homomorphic Encryption on TPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14718)
*Shruthi Gorantala,Jianming Tong,Asra Ali,Baiyu Li,Jonathan Katz,Jeremy Kun,Thomas Steinke,Abhradeep Thakurta,Julian Walker,Amir Yazdanbakhsh*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出AlphaEvolve，一种利用LLM驱动代码生成和进化搜索自动探索密码学内核硬件优化方法的系统。在Google Cloud TPUv5e上，AlphaEvolve在24小时内将TFHE引导延迟提升2.5倍，CKKS旋转和乘法延迟分别提升1.31倍和1.18倍，超越了人工优化的现有最优方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全同态加密（FHE）因计算开销巨大难以大规模部署。虽然TPU等硬件加速器可以缓解这一问题，但将复杂密码学内核映射到此类架构上非常困难，现有编译器栈对底层硬件利用率抽象不足，开发者只能通过手动试错进行优化，导致执行碎片化和资源利用率低下。

Method: 将硬件感知的密码学内核优化建模为进化搜索问题，利用AlphaEvolve闭环系统，通过LLM驱动代码生成自动产生优化候选，并使用真实的硬件执行反馈和正确性测试来指导搜索进化过程。

Result: 在Google Cloud TPUv5e上，针对TFHE（Jaxite）和CKKS（CROSS）两种FHE方案的基元进行评估。24小时内，TFHE引导延迟提升2.5倍，CKKS旋转和乘法延迟分别提升1.31倍和1.18倍，均优于人工设计的最优方案。

Conclusion: AlphaEvolve能够自动探索密码学内核在硬件加速器上的优化空间，帮助研究人员在密码学、编译器和硬件加速器之间权衡优化，显著加速FHE的硬件部署。

Abstract: The deployment of Fully Homomorphic Encryption (FHE) at scale is hindered due to its heavy computational overhead. While specialized hardware accelerators like Google Tensor Processing Units (TPUs) can help, mapping complex cryptographic kernels onto such architectures remains a challenge. Efficient execution requires co-optimization between the systolic array-based Matrix Multiplication Unit (MXU) and Vector Processing Units (VPUs), as well as the orchestration of data movement across the vector register files. Existing compiler stacks often abstract low-level hardware utilization, requiring developers to adopt a manual trial-and-error process that often results in fragmented execution and underutilized resources. To accelerate this development process, we use AlphaEvolve to automate the exploration of hardware-aware cryptographic-kernel optimizations. We frame optimization as an evolutionary search problem, utilizing the closed-loop system provided by AlphaEvolve, that leverages LLM-driven code generation. We use real-world feedback from hardware execution and rigorous correctness testing to guide the evolution process. We evaluate AlphaEvolve optimization on primitives for both the TFHE (Jaxite) and CKKS (CROSS) FHE schemes on Google Cloud TPUv5e, a contemporary TPU architecture. Within 24 hours of automated exploration, AlphaEvolve discovered implementation-level optimizations that improve TFHE bootstrap latency by 2.5x and CKKS rotation and multiplication latency by 1.31x and 1.18x, respectively, relative to human-engineered state of the art. These results demonstrate that AlphaEvolve can be used to enable researchers to navigate the optimization trade-offs between cryptography, compilers, and hardware accelerators.

</details>


### [197] [EVA: Editing for Versatile Alignment against Jailbreaks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14750)
*Yi Wang,Hongye Qiu,Yue Xu,Sibei Yang,Zhan Qin,Minlie Huang,Wenjie Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: EVA是一个通过直接模型编辑实现安全对齐的新框架，能够精准定位并修改导致模型易受越狱攻击的特定神经元，在不影响模型通用推理能力的前提下有效防御越狱攻击，且计算开销低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全对齐方法（安全微调、外部过滤器）存在计算开销大和安全-效用权衡问题，即在防御有害内容的同时会降低模型在良性任务上的性能。

Method: EVA将安全对齐重新定义为精确的知识修正任务，通过识别并手术式编辑对有害指令敏感的特定神经元，实现局部参数更新，保留大部分模型参数不变。

Result: 大量实验表明EVA在LLMs和VLMs上均优于基线方法，能够有效缓解越狱攻击，同时保持模型通用推理能力不受影响。

Conclusion: EVA为后部署阶段的安全对齐提供了一种精准且高效的解决方案，开创性地将直接模型编辑应用于安全对齐领域。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) and Vision Language Models (VLMs) have demonstrated impressive capabilities but remain vulnerable to jailbreaking attacks, where adversaries exploit textual or visual triggers to bypass safety guardrails. Recent defenses typically rely on safety fine-tuning or external filters to reduce the model's likelihood of producing harmful content. While effective to some extent, these methods often incur significant computational overheads and suffer from the safety utility trade-off, degrading the model's performance on benign tasks. To address these challenges, we propose EVA (Editing for Versatile Alignment against Jailbreaks), a novel framework that pioneers the application of direct model editing for safety alignment. EVA reframes safety alignment as a precise knowledge correction task. Instead of retraining massive parameters, EVA identifies and surgically edits specific neurons responsible for the model's susceptibility to harmful instructions, while leaving the vast majority of the model unchanged. By localizing the updates, EVA effectively neutralizes harmful behaviors without compromising the model's general reasoning capabilities. Extensive experiments demonstrate that EVA outperforms baselines in mitigating jailbreaks across both LLMs and VLMs, offering a precise and efficient solution for post-deployment safety alignment.

</details>


### [198] [Do Coding Agents Understand Least-Privilege Authorization?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14859)
*Zheng Yan,Jingxiang Weng,Charles Chen,Dengyun Peng,Ethan Qin,Jiannan Guan,Jinhao Liu,Qiming Yu,Yixin Yuan,Fanqing Meng,Carl Che,Mengkang Hu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出权限边界推断任务和AuthBench基准测试，发现现有模型在授权策略生成上存在瓶颈，并提出了充分性-严格性分解方法来改进安全授权。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编程智能体在访问shell、仓库和用户文件时，需要最小权限授权以确保安全部署，但当前模型难以自主推断任务所需的权限边界。

Method: 1) 提出权限边界推断任务，让模型根据任务指令和终端环境生成文件级别的读/写/执行策略；2) 构建AuthBench基准测试（120个真实终端任务，含人工审核的权限标签和可执行验证器）；3) 提出充分性-严格性分解方法：先生成覆盖导向策略，再审计每个权限条目的合理性和敏感性。

Result: 前沿模型在权限分配上存在矛盾：既遗漏执行链所需的权限，又授予未使用或敏感权限。增加推理时间不会解决此问题，每个模型会趋向于自身的"权限吸引子"（要么宽泛但暴露，要么严格但脆弱）。充分性-严格性分解方法在严格偏好的模型上提升了最高15.8%的敏感任务成功率，同时降低了所有评估模型的攻击成功率。

Conclusion: 直接策略生成是瓶颈所在，因为单次生成既要发现所有必要权限又要拒绝所有不必要权限。分解方法通过先模拟任务生成覆盖策略再审计敏感性，有效解决了这一矛盾。

Abstract: As coding agents gain access to shells, repositories, and user files, least-privilege authorization becomes a prerequisite for safe deployment: an agent should receive enough authority to complete the task, without unnecessary authority that exposes sensitive surfaces.To study whether current models can infer this boundary themselves, we first introduce permission-boundary inference, where a model maps a task instruction and terminal environment to a file-level read/write/execute policy, and AuthBench, a benchmark of 120 realistic terminal tasks with human-reviewed permission labels and executable validators for utility and attack outcomes.AuthBench shows that authorization is not a simple conservative-versus-permissive calibration problem: frontier models often omit permissions required by the execution chain while also granting unused or sensitive accesses.Increasing inference-time reasoning does not resolve this mismatch. Instead, each model moves toward a model-specific authorization attractor: more reasoning makes it more consistent in its own failure mode, whether broad-but-exposed or tight-but-brittle.This suggests that direct policy generation is the bottleneck, because a single generation must both discover all necessary accesses and reject all unnecessary ones.We therefore propose Sufficiency-Tightness Decomposition, which first generates a coverage-oriented policy by forward-simulating the task and then audits each granted entry for grounding and sensitivity.Across tested models, this decomposition improves sensitive-task success by up to 15.8% on tightness-biased models while reducing attack success across all evaluated models.

</details>


### [199] [PickleFuzzer: A Case Study in Fuzzing for Discrepancies Between Python Pickle Implementations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15084)
*Justin Applegate,Andreas Kellas*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出PickleFuzzer，一种基于生成的自定义模糊测试工具，用于发现Python三种原生pickle实现之间的不一致性。该工具通过语法生成pickle对象，进行差异测试，检测到14个新不一致，其中4个可绕过Hugging Face等平台的安全扫描工具，已报告至Python软件基金会并获得$750赏金。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Python的pickle序列化协议虽然强大但存在安全风险，现有缓解措施（如限制导入、静态/动态分析工具）依赖于对Pickle虚拟机（PVM）操作码的准确解释，而Python三种原生PVM模块之间的差异可能导致对恶意载荷的检测失准，削弱现有防御机制。

Method: PickleFuzzer采用基于生成的模糊测试方法：首先根据缺失的pickle规范开发语法规则生成pickle对象；然后将生成的pickle对象分别传递给三种pickle实现；最后通过比较各实现在执行过程中抛出的异常或关键内部状态的变化来检测不一致性，无需依赖规范驱动的oracle。

Result: 发现了14个新的不一致性，其中4个关键不一致可被利用来绕过Hugging Face等模型托管平台部署的安全扫描工具。

Conclusion: 差异测试是识别重要pickle实现中安全相关不一致性的可行方法，该工作可为未来通过更有针对性的模糊测试发现更深层pickle bug提供方向。

Abstract: Python's native serialization protocol, pickle, is a powerful but insecure format for transferring untrusted data. It is frequently used, especially for saving machine learning models, despite known security challenges. While developers sometimes mitigate this risk by restricting imports during unpickling or using static and dynamic analysis tools, these approaches are error-prone and depend heavily on accurate interpretations of the Pickle Virtual Machine (PVM) opcodes. Discrepancies across Python's three native PVM modules can lead to incorrect detection of malicious payloads and undermine existing defenses.
  To efficiently and scalably identify discrepancies, we present PickleFuzzer, a custom generation-based fuzzer that identifies inconsistencies across pickle implementations. PickleFuzzer generates pickle objects, passes them to each implementation, and detects differences in thrown exceptions or changes to key internal states. It generates pickle objects using a grammar, which we developed to account for the missing pickle specification. It determines discrepancies by comparing the execution behaviors of each test implementation, rather than requiring a specification-derived oracle. PickleFuzzer detected 14 new discrepancies between the pickle implementations. Four discrepancies are critical and can be used to bypass security-critical scanning tools like those deployed on the popular model hosting platform, Hugging Face. We disclosed all findings to the Python Software Foundation for remediation, and additionally disclosed the security issues to a bug bounty platform and were awarded a $750 bounty. We demonstrate that differential testing is a viable approach for identifying security-relevant discrepancies in important pickle implementations, and our work can lead to promising future directions for finding deeper pickle bugs with more directed fuzzing.

</details>


### [200] [Talk is (Not) Cheap: A Taxonomy and Benchmark Coverage Audit for LLM Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15118)
*Karthik Raghu Iyer,Yazdan Jamshidi,Nicholas Bray,Alexey A. Shvets*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于STRIDE模型的4×6目标×技术矩阵框架，用于审计LLM攻击基准测试的覆盖范围。该框架从932篇arXiv安全研究中提取了507个叶节点的推理时攻击分类体系，发现现有六大基准测试中三大主要框架（HarmBench、InjecAgent、AgentDojo）覆盖了最多25%的矩阵，且整个STRIDE威胁类别（服务中断、模型内部机制）缺乏标准化评估，尽管已发表的攻击在这些类别中实现了46倍令牌放大和96%的攻击成功率。同时发现命名碎片化严重（单个攻击最多29种表面形式）和安全与对齐绕过高度集中。分类体系、攻击记录和覆盖映射作为可扩展工件发布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM攻击基准测试缺乏统一的覆盖范围评估标准，导致无法系统性地识别评估空白区域。作者希望建立一个可复用的审计框架，使社区能够追踪评估缺口是否在填补。

Method: 从932篇arXiv安全研究（2023-2026年）中提取推理时攻击，构建了507个叶节点的分类体系（401个数据填充+106个威胁模型推导），并基于STRIDE模型设计了一个4×6的目标×技术矩阵。通过将六个公开基准测试映射到该矩阵，进行覆盖范围的基准外部验证。

Result: 三大主要基准测试框架仅覆盖矩阵最多25%且完全不重叠；服务中断和模型内部机制等STRIDE威胁类别完全缺乏标准化评估；发现了2521个独特攻击组中严重的命名碎片化现象（单个攻击最多29种表面形式）；安全与对齐绕过类别高度集中。

Conclusion: 现有的LLM攻击基准测试对威胁面覆盖严重不足，存在系统性评估空白。作者发布的分类体系、攻击记录和覆盖映射工件可作为可扩展标准，帮助社区追踪评估缺口是否随新基准出现而逐步关闭。

Abstract: We introduce a reusable framework for auditing whether LLM attack benchmarks collectively cover the threat surface: a 4$\times$6 Target $\times$ Technique matrix grounded in STRIDE, constructed from a 507-leaf taxonomy -- 401 data-populated and 106 threat-model-derived leaves -- of inference-time attacks extracted from 932 arXiv security studies (2023--2026). The matrix enables benchmark-external validation -- auditing collective coverage rather than individual benchmark consistency. Applying it to six public benchmarks reveals that the three primary frameworks (HarmBench, InjecAgent, AgentDojo) occupy non-overlapping cells covering at most 25\% of the matrix, while entire STRIDE threat categories (Service Disruption, Model Internals) lack any standardized evaluation, despite published attacks in these categories achieving 46$\times$ token amplification and 96\% attack success rates through mechanisms which no benchmark tests. The corpus of 2,521 unique attack groups further reveals pervasive naming fragmentation (up to 29 surface forms for a single attack) and heavy concentration in Safety \& Alignment Bypass, structural properties invisible at smaller scale. The taxonomy, attack records, and coverage mappings are released as extensible artifacts; as new benchmarks emerge, they can be mapped onto the same matrix, enabling the community to track whether evaluation gaps are closing.

</details>


### [201] [MetaBackdoor: Exploiting Positional Encoding as a Backdoor Attack Surface in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15172)
*Rui Wen,Mark Russinovich,Andrew Paverd,Jun Sakuma,Ahmed Salem*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出MetaBackdoor，一种利用位置编码（而非文本内容）作为触发器的后门攻击方法，揭示了Transformer大语言模型中位置信息可作为新的攻击面，并展示了长度触发、信息泄露、自我激活等新型攻击能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM后门攻击主要依赖基于内容的触发器（如特定词语），但作者认为这一假设是不必要且有限的。位置信息作为Transformer架构中必然存在的编码信号，尚未被充分研究为攻击面。

Method: 提出MetaBackdoor，利用Transformer中位置编码的特性，将长度相关的序列位置结构作为触发信号。具体包括：1) 长度触发的简单位置后门；2) 组合内容触发器的正交后门；3) 多轮对话中的自我激活场景。

Result: 实验表明：1) 仅使用文本长度作为触发器即可激活后门；2) 可诱导模型泄露敏感内部信息（如系统提示）；3) 正常多轮交互可自然进入触发区域并触发恶意行为；4) 与内容后门正交组合可实现更精确的触发条件。

Conclusion: 位置编码是大语言模型中此前被忽视的攻击面，扩展了后门攻击的威胁模型。现有基于文本检测的防御策略不足以应对此类攻击，需要新的防御方案。

Abstract: Backdoor attacks pose a serious security threat to large language models (LLMs), which are increasingly deployed as general-purpose assistants in safety- and privacy-critical applications. Existing LLM backdoors rely primarily on content-based triggers, requiring explicit modification of the input text. In this work, we show that this assumption is unnecessary and limiting. We introduce MetaBackdoor, a new class of backdoor attacks that exploits positional information as the trigger, without modifying textual content. Our key insight is that Transformer-based LLMs necessarily encode token positions to process ordered sequences. As a result, length-correlated positional structure is reflected in the model's internal computation and can be used as an effective non-content trigger signal.
  We demonstrate that even a simple length-based positional trigger is sufficient to activate stealthy backdoors. Unlike prior attacks, MetaBackdoor operates on visibly and semantically clean inputs and enables qualitatively new capabilities. We show that a backdoored LLM can be induced to disclose sensitive internal information, including proprietary system prompts, once a length condition is satisfied. We further demonstrate a self-activation scenario, where normal multi-turn interaction can move the conversation context into the trigger region and induce malicious tool-call behavior without attacker-supplied trigger text. In addition, MetaBackdoor is orthogonal to content-based backdoors and can be composed with them to create more precise and harder-to-detect activation conditions.
  Our results expand the threat model of LLM backdoors by revealing positional encoding as a previously overlooked attack surface. This challenges defenses that focus on detecting suspicious text and highlights the need for new defense strategies that explicitly account for positional triggers in modern LLM architectures.

</details>


### [202] [Capacitive Touchscreens at Risk: Recovering Handwritten Trajectory on Smartphone via Electromagnetic Emanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.11484)
*Yukun Cheng,Shiyu Zhu,Changhai Ou,Xingshuo Han,Yuan Li,Shihui Zheng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文发现并利用电容触摸屏的电磁侧信道漏洞，提出TESLA攻击框架，通过非接触方式捕获书写时产生的电磁信号，实时恢复二维手写轨迹。在多种商用手机上实现77%字符识别准确率和0.74的Jaccard指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示电容触摸屏存在电磁侧信道安全漏洞，证明该漏洞可被利用来恢复精细、连续的手写轨迹，威胁用户隐私安全。

Method: 提出TESLA非接触攻击框架，捕获屏幕书写时产生的电磁信号，通过回归方法将信号实时映射为二维手写轨迹。

Result: 在多种商用手机上测试，字符识别准确率达77%，Jaccard指数为0.74，轨迹与原始手写高度相似。

Conclusion: 电容触摸屏的电磁侧信道存在严重安全风险，攻击者可在非接触条件下有效恢复手写内容，需引起重视并采取防护措施。

Abstract: This paper reveals and exploits a critical security vulnerability: the electromagnetic (EM) side channel of capacitive touchscreens leaks sufficient information to recover fine-grained, continuous handwriting trajectories. We present Touchscreen Electromagnetic Side-channel Leakage Attack (TESLA), a non-contact attack framework that captures EM signals generated during on-screen writing and regresses them into two-dimensional (2D) handwriting trajectories in real time. Extensive evaluations across a variety of commercial off-the-shelf (COTS) smartphones show that TESLA achieves 77% character recognition accuracy and a Jaccard index of 0.74, demonstrating its capability to recover highly recognizable motion trajectories that closely resemble the original handwriting under realistic attack conditions.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [203] [Average Gradient Outer Product in kernel regression provably recovers the central subspace for multi-index models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15082)
*Libin Zhu,Damek Davis,Dmitriy Drusvyatskiy,Maryam Fazel*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究核岭回归（KRR）的Average Gradient Outer Product（AGOP）在样本量不足以保证准确预测时，仍能恢复低维中心子空间的问题。当目标函数f*(x)=h(Ux)具有低度p成分时，子空间恢复仅需n≍d^{p+δ}样本（δ∈(0,1)），远低于准确预测所需的n≍d^{p*}样本，揭示了预测与表征之间的分离现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在样本量不足以实现准确预测的情况下，学习器能否发现数据中有效的低维结构，并解释迭代核方法（如RFM）在实践中样本高效的原理。

Method: 对核岭回归拟合的预测器计算Average Gradient Outer Product（AGOP），提取其前r维特征空间来恢复中心子空间；理论分析证明在目标函数存在低度p成分时，子空间恢复所需样本量远低于准确预测所需样本量。

Result: 在目标函数f*具有度数p*的情况下，KRR准确预测需要n≍d^{p*}样本；而若f*的低度p成分已包含所有相关方向，则子空间恢复仅需n≍d^{p+δ}（δ∈(0,1)），实现了预测与表征之间的样本复杂度分离。

Conclusion: 证明了预测误差大时仍能可靠恢复低维结构，解释了迭代核方法（如RFM）的样本高效性，揭示了表征学习与预测任务之间的根本性分离。

Abstract: We study a prototypical situation when a learned predictor can discover useful low-dimensional structure in data, while using fewer samples than are needed for accurate prediction. Specifically, we consider the problem of recovering a multi-index polynomial $f^*(x)=h(Ux)$, with $U\in\mathbb{R}^{r\times d}$ and $r\ll d$, from finitely many data/label pairs. Importantly, the target function depends on input $x$ only through the projection onto an unknown $r$-dimensional central subspace. The algorithm we analyze is appealingly simple: fit kernel ridge regression (KRR) to the data and compute the Average Gradient Outer Product (AGOP) from the fitted predictor. Our main results show that under reasonable assumptions the top $r$-dimensional eigenspace of AGOP provably recovers the central subspace, even in regimes when the prediction error remains large. Specifically, if the target function $f^*$ has degree $p^*$, it is known that $n\asymp d^{p^*}$ samples are necessary for KRR to achieve accurate prediction. In contrast, we show that if a low degree $p$ component of $f^*$ already carries all relevant directions for prediction, subspace recovery occurs in the much lower sample regime $n\asymp d^{p+δ}$ for any $δ\in(0,1)$. Our results thus demonstrate a separation between prediction and representation, and provide an explanation for why iterative kernel methods such as Recursive Feature Machines (RFM) can be sample-efficient in practice.

</details>


### [204] [Covariance-aware sampling for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13910)
*Andrea Schioppa,Tim Salimans*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出了一种协方差感知采样器，通过显式建模反向过程协方差并使用Tweedie公式与傅里叶空间分解，在少步数条件下显著提升像素空间扩散模型的采样质量，仅需额外一次JVP计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有采样器在少步数条件下仅依赖反向分布的预测均值，忽略了协方差信息，导致采样质量下降。本文旨在通过显式建模协方差来改善这一问题。

Method: 结合Tweedie公式估计协方差，并使用结构化傅里叶空间分解来高效表示协方差矩阵；作为DDIM的扩展，每个采样步仅增加一次Jacobian-Vector Product (JVP)计算。

Result: 在相同NFE条件下，该方法在像素基DM上一致优于Heun、DPM-Solver++等二阶采样器及aDDIM采样器。

Conclusion: 显式建模反向过程协方差是提升少步数扩散模型采样质量的有效途径，且计算开销极低。

Abstract: We present a covariance-aware sampler that improves the quality of pixel-space Diffusion Model (DM) sampling in the few-step regime. We hypothesize that in the few-step regime samplers fail because they rely solely on the predicted mean of the reverse distribution, while our solution explicitly models the reverse-process covariance. Our method combines Tweedie's formula to estimate the covariance with an efficient, structured Fourier-space decomposition of the covariance matrix. Implemented as an extension of DDIM, our method requires only a minimal overhead: one extra Jacobian-Vector Product (JVP) per step. We demonstrate that for pixel-based DMs, our method consistently produces superior samples compared to state-of-the-art second order samplers (Heun, DPM-Solver++) and the recent aDDIM sampler, at an identical number of function evaluations (NFE).

</details>


### [205] [Multi-Scale Dequant: Eliminating Dequantization Bottleneck via Activation Decomposition for Efficient LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13915)
*Lingchao Zheng,Yuwei Fan,Jun Li,Chengqiu Hu,Qichen Liao,Junyi Fan,Rui Shi,Fangzheng Miao*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出多尺度反量化框架MSD，通过将高精度激活分解为多个低精度分量，直接与量化权重进行矩阵乘法，从而消除LLM推理中反量化操作带来的计算瓶颈，在Ascend NPU等架构上实现了更高的计算吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在现代AI加速器（如Ascend NPU）上，反量化操作（将低比特权重转换回高精度）已成为LLM推理的关键瓶颈，其计算周期甚至超过矩阵乘法本身，导致高吞吐张量核利用率不足。

Method: MSD将高精度BF16激活分解为多个低精度分量，每个分量直接与量化权重通过硬件加速的原生GEMM相乘，从而将计算范式从精度转换转变为多尺度近似，避免了GEMM前的INT8到BF16权重转换。

Result: 对于W4A16的INT8权重，两遍INT8分解达到近16有效比特位；对于MXFP4权重，两遍MXFP4分解达到近6.6有效比特位（误差界1/64），超过单遍MXFP8的5.24比特位。MSD消除了反量化导致的Vector-Cube流水线停顿，并降低了注意力中KV缓存HBM流量高达2.5倍。

Conclusion: 数值仿真表明MSD在矩阵乘法和Flash Attention核上相比反量化基线不降低精度，且在多数设置下实现更低的L2误差。

Abstract: Quantization is essential for efficient large language model (LLM) inference, yet the dequantization step-converting low-bit weights back to high-precision for matrix multiplication has become a critical bottleneck on modern AI accelerators. On architectures with decoupled compute units (e.g., Ascend NPUs), dequantization operations can consume more cycles than the matrix multiplication itself, leaving the high-throughput tensor cores underutilized. This paper presents Multi-Scale Dequant (MSD), a quantization framework that removes weight/KV dequantization from the GEMM critical path. Instead of lifting low-bit weights to BF16 precision, MSD decomposes high-precision BF16 activations into multiple low-precision components, each of which can be multiplied directly with quantized weights via native hardware-accelerated GEMM. This approach shifts the computational paradigm from precision conversion to multi-scale approximation, avoiding INT8-to-BF16 weight conversion before GEMM. We instantiate MSD for two weight formats and derive tight error bounds for each. For INT8 weights (W4A16), two-pass INT8 decomposition achieves near 16 effective bits. For MXFP4 weights (W4A16), two-pass MXFP4 decomposition yields near 6.6 effective bits with error bound 1/64 per block surpassing single-pass MXFP8(5.24 bits) while maintaining the same effective GEMM compute time. We further derive closed-form latency and HBM traffic models showing that MSD avoids the Vector-Cube pipeline stall caused by dequantization and reduces KV cache HBM traffic by up to 2.5 times in attention. Numerical simulations on matrix multiplication and Flash Attention kernels confirm that MSD does not degrade accuracy compared to dequantization baselines, and in many settings achieves lower L2 error.

</details>


### [206] [A Regret Perspective on Online Multiple Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13916)
*Qingyang Hao,Kongchang Zhou,Fang Kong,Hongxin Wei*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出加权遗憾（Weighted Regret）统一评估在线多重检验中的假阳性与假阴性成本，揭示确定性FDR控制方法的线性遗憾下界，并提出Decoupled-OMT（DOMT）元包装器，通过历史解耦的随机扰动实现阶数最优的亚线性遗憾降低，在非平稳环境中有效缓解阈值枯竭问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统在线多重检验（OMT）孤立评估FDR与统计功效，掩盖了现代自动化流程中假阳性和假阴性高度不对称的成本，需要统一的评估框架。

Method: 提出加权遗憾指标；证明遗憾守恒对偶性；设计Decoupled-OMT（DOMT）元包装器，引入历史解耦的严格非负随机扰动来缓解阈值枯竭；推导冷启动税（Cold-Start Tax）刻画算法优势的相变。

Result: 确定性FDR控制过程必然遭受Ω(T)线性遗憾；DOMT在平稳环境中保持精确渐近安全性，在突发环境中实现阶数最优的Ω(√T)遗憾降低；实验验证DOMT持续减少经验加权遗憾，实现阶数最优的亚线性阈值枯竭缓解。

Conclusion: DOMT作为基线无关的元包装器，能有效拯救纯确定性基线免受严重阈值枯竭，保证零额外假阴性，并在非平稳帕累托前沿上实现最优权衡。

Abstract: Online Multiple Testing (OMT), a fundamental pillar of sequential statistical inference, traditionally evaluates the False Discovery Rate (FDR) and statistical power in isolation, obscuring the highly asymmetric costs of false positives and false negatives in modern automated pipelines. To unify this evaluation, we introduce $\textit{Weighted Regret}$. Under this metric, we prove the $\textit{Duality of Regret Conservation}$: purely deterministic procedures ensuring strict FDR control inevitably incur an $Ω(T)$ linear regret penalty, as threshold depletion during signal-sparse cold starts forces massive false negatives. Tailored for exogenous testing streams, we propose Decoupled-OMT (DOMT) as a baseline-agnostic meta-wrapper. By incorporating a history-decoupled, strictly non-negative random perturbation, DOMT rescues purely deterministic baselines from severe threshold depletion. Crucially, it preserves exact asymptotic safety in stationary environments and rigorously bounds finite-sample error inflation during cold-starts. Guaranteeing zero additional false negatives, it yields an order-optimal $Ω(\sqrt{T})$ regret reduction in bursty environments, with a derived ``Cold-Start Tax'' characterizing the exact phase transition of algorithmic superiority. Experiments validate that DOMT consistently curtails empirical weighted regret, achieving an order-optimal sublinear mitigation of threshold depletion to navigate the non-stationary Pareto frontier.

</details>


### [207] [To discretize continually: Mean shift interacting particle systems for Bayesian inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14142)
*Ayoub Belhadji,Daniel Sharp,Youssef M. Marzouk*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过交互粒子系统最小化最大均值差异（MMD）来构建加权样本集（求积规则）的新方法，用于近似未知归一化密度的期望。该方法扩展了均值漂移算法和最优量化算法，具有对归一化常数不变、支持无梯度和基于梯度的实现、快速收敛、能处理各向异性和多模态、避免模式坍塌、可扩展至高维等优点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯推断等领域中，经常需要根据未归一化的概率密度计算期望，这是一个核心但困难的任务。现有方法（如MCMC、变分推断）存在收敛慢、模式坍塌、难以处理高维等问题，亟需更高效、稳健的近似方法。

Method: 通过交互粒子系统最小化目标分布与加权样本集之间的最大均值差异（MMD），将经典的均值漂移算法和最优量化算法扩展到连续分布情形。算法设计保证对未知归一化常数不变，并提供无梯度和基于梯度两种实现方式。

Result: 该方法在多种基准测试中表现优异，包括多模态混合分布、贝叶斯层次模型、PDE约束反问题等，展现出快速收敛、各向异性和多模态适应性、无模式坍塌、高维可扩展性等优势。

Conclusion: 所提出的均值漂移交互粒子系统是一种高效、稳健的求积规则构建方法，在贝叶斯推断及相关领域具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: Integration against a probability distribution given its unnormalized density is a central task in Bayesian inference and other fields. We introduce new methods for approximating such expectations with a small set of weighted samples -- i.e., a quadrature rule -- constructed via an interacting particle system that minimizes maximum mean discrepancy (MMD) to the target distribution. These methods extend the classical mean shift algorithm, as well as recent algorithms for optimal quantization of empirical distributions, to the case of continuous distributions. Crucially, our approach creates dynamics for MMD minimization that are invariant to the unknown normalizing constant; they also admit both gradient-free and gradient-informed implementations. The resulting mean shift interacting particle systems converge quickly, capture anisotropy and multi-modality, avoid mode collapse, and scale to high dimensions. We demonstrate their performance on a wide range of benchmark sampling problems, including multi-modal mixtures, Bayesian hierarchical models, PDE-constrained inverse problems, and beyond.

</details>


### [208] [On the Burden of Achieving Fairness in Conformal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14260)
*Ziang Gao,Pengqi Liu,Archer Yi Yang,Mouloud Belbahri,Jesse C. Cresswell,Masoud Asgharian*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了分裂共形校准中池化阈值导致的组间覆盖率扭曲现象。推导了守恒律和下界，证明池化校准会产生不可归约的组间覆盖率差异；发现Equalized Coverage和Equalized Set Size两种公平性定义存在根本性矛盾；量化了分组策略与池化策略之间的代价。实验证实了覆盖率和集合大小维度之间的双向权衡，表明校准选择不会消除组间异质性，而是决定扭曲出现在哪个维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共形预测常用单一池化阈值进行校准，但这种方法会隐藏不同组别分数分布的异质性，导致组间覆盖率失真。作者旨在从理论上分析这一现象的本质，并揭示不同公平性定义之间的内在矛盾。

Method: 通过分裂共形校准背后的总体分数分布进行理论分析：推导守恒律和下界；证明两种公平性定义（Equalized Coverage和Equalized Set Size）的张力关系；量化分组与池化策略之间的代价；并在合成数据和真实数据上进行有限样本校准实验验证。

Result: 池化校准产生的组间覆盖率失真规模由跨组分位数异质性决定；Equalized Coverage和Equalized Set Size两种公平性定义存在根本性矛盾；分组和池化策略之间存在可量化的双向权衡；实验验证了理论发现。

Conclusion: 校准选择不消除跨组异质性，而是决定扭曲出现在覆盖率维度还是集合大小维度，这为实践中分析公平性导向的校准选择提供了原则性视角。

Abstract: Conformal prediction is often calibrated with a single pooled threshold, but this can hide cross-group heterogeneity in score distributions and distort group-wise coverage. We study this phenomenon through the population score distributions underlying split conformal calibration. First, we derive a conservation law and lower bound showing that pooled calibration incurs irreducible group-wise coverage distortion at a scale set by cross-group quantile heterogeneity. Second, we demonstrate that the two leading fairness definitions for conformal prediction, Equalized Coverage and Equalized Set Size, are fundamentally in tension. Third, we quantify the cost of moving between policies which treat groups separately or pool them. Experiments on synthetic and real data confirm the same bidirectional trade-off after finite-sample calibration. Our results show that, for the policy families studied here, calibration choice does not remove cross-group heterogeneity; it determines whether the resulting distortion appears in the coverage or size dimension, providing a principled lens for analyzing fairness-oriented calibration choices in practice.

</details>


### [209] [Training-Free Generative Sampling via Moment-Matched Score Smoothing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14276)
*Zhenyu Yao,Daniel Paulin*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出MM-SOLD方法，一种无需训练、基于矩匹配的交互式粒子采样器，通过在整个采样轨迹中强制执行目标矩来实现快速鲁棒的采样，在CPU上即可获得与神经扩散基线相竞争的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型训练成本高昂，而现有研究表明分数匹配隐式地平滑了经验分数，这种平滑偏差通过捕捉低维数据几何结构来促进泛化。受此启发，作者希望设计一种无需训练的高效采样方法。

Method: 提出矩匹配分数平滑过阻尼朗之万动力学（MM-SOLD），一种无需训练的交互式粒子采样器，在整个采样轨迹中强制执行目标矩。在大粒子数极限下，证明了经验粒子密度收敛到确定性极限，其单粒子平稳边际是通过指数倾斜朴素分数平滑扩散目标得到的吉布斯-玻尔兹曼分布，该分布的均值和协方差与训练数据的经验矩一致。

Result: 在2D分布和潜空间图像生成实验中，MM-SOLD能够在CPU上实现快速、鲁棒、无需训练的采样，样本保真度和多样性与神经扩散基线相当。

Conclusion: MM-SOLD证明了通过矩匹配可以在无需训练的情况下实现与神经扩散模型竞争的性能，为高效采样提供了新思路。

Abstract: Diffusion models generate samples by denoising along the score of a perturbed target distribution. In practice, one trains a neural diffusion model, which is computationally expensive. Recent work suggests that score matching implicitly smooths the empirical score, and that this smoothing bias promotes generalization by capturing low-dimensional data geometry. We propose moment-matched score-smoothed overdamped Langevin dynamics (MM-SOLD), a training-free interacting particle sampler that enforces the target moments throughout the sampling trajectory. We prove that, in the large-particle limit, the empirical particle density converges to a deterministic limit whose one-particle stationary marginal is a Gibbs--Boltzmann density obtained by exponentially tilting a naive score-smoothed diffusion target. The mean and covariance of this distribution agree with the empirical moments of the training data. Experiments on 2D distributions and latent-space image generation show that MM-SOLD enables fast, robust, training-free sampling on CPUs, with sample fidelity and diversity competitive with neural diffusion baselines.

</details>


### [210] [Large Dimensional Kernel Ridge Regression: Extending to Product Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14524)
*Yang Zhou,Yicheng Li,Yuqian Cheng,Qian Lin*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文建立了一类新的高维核函数族，推导了相应的泛化误差收敛率，恢复了之前仅在内积核球面上观察到的关键现象，包括：当源条件s≤1时的极小化最优性、s>1时的饱和效应、收敛速率中的周期性平台现象以及样本量n的多重下降行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于高维核岭回归中饱和效应和多重下降行为的研究大多局限于内积核球面或强特征函数假设（如超压缩性），对于其他核函数是否也存在类似行为仍是开放问题。本文旨在建立更广泛的核函数族，以推广这些现象。

Method: 建立了一类新的高维核函数族，推导了相应的泛化误差收敛率，通过理论分析恢复并验证了关键现象。

Result: 恢复了三个关键现象：1) 源条件s≤1时的极小化最优性；2) s>1时的饱和效应；3) 收敛率中的周期性平台现象以及样本量n的多重下降行为。

Conclusion: 本文建立的新核函数族能够推广先前仅在内积核球面上观察到的饱和效应和多重下降行为，为高维核岭回归的理论提供了更广泛的框架。

Abstract: Recent studies have reported $\textit{saturation effects}$ and $\textit{multiple descent behavior}$ in large dimensional kernel ridge regression (KRR). However, these findings are predominantly derived under restrictive settings, such as inner product kernels on sphere or strong eigenfunction assumptions like hypercontractivity. Whether such behaviors hold for other kernels remains an open question. In this paper, we establish a broad, new family of large dimensional kernels and derive the corresponding convergence rates of the generalization error. As a result, we recover key phenomena previously associated with inner product kernels on sphere, including: $i)$ the $\textit{minimax optimality}$ when the source condition $s\le 1$; $ii)$ the $\textit{saturation effect}$ when $s>1$; $iii)$ a $\textit{periodic plateau phenomenon}$ in the convergence rate and a $\textit {multiple-descent behavior}$ with respect to the sample size $n$.

</details>


### [211] [K-Models: a Flexible and Interpretable Method for Ordinal Clustering with Application to Antigen-Antibody Interaction Profiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14828)
*Giulia Patanè,Alessandra Menafoglio,Alexander Krauth,Peter Fechner,Luca Dede',Bianca Maria Colosimo,Federica Nicolussi*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出K-Models框架，通过引入序数约束和估计随机过程的关键潜在要素，在保持与现有技术相当性能的同时，显著提升了函数型数据聚类的可解释性和结构识别能力，并在生物分子相互作用监测的ROI曲线上进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有函数型数据聚类方法过度关注划分精度而忽视可解释性，当数据生成过程具有特定底层结构且聚类间存在序数关系时，难以提取有意义的洞见。

Method: 提出K-Models框架，将序数约束整合到聚类过程中，并估计生成观测函数型数据的随机过程的关键底层元素。

Result: 通过仿真实验和真实应用（特别是ROI曲线——来自反射式传感器的抗原-抗体结合反应曲线）验证，K-Models在可解释性上优于现有方法，同时性能与最先进技术相当。

Conclusion: K-Models通过将结构假设融入聚类过程，为分析具有底层序数结构的函数型数据提供了有价值的工具，在可解释性和性能之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Existing clustering methods for functional data often prioritize partitioning accuracy over interpretability, making it challenging to extract meaningful insights when the data-generating process follows a specific underlying structure and an ordinal relationship among clusters is suspected. This work introduces K-Models, a novel framework that integrates ordinal constraints and estimates key underlying elements of the random process generating the observed functional profiles, improving both interpretability and structure identification. The proposed method is evaluated through simulations and real-world applications. In particular, it is tested on Region of Interest (ROI) curves, which represent reaction profiles from a reflectometric sensor monitoring biomolecular interactions, such as antigen-antibody binding. These curves represent changes in reflected light intensity over time at multiple measurement spots with immobilized antigens during analyte exposure, capturing the binding dynamics of the system. The goal is to identify intrinsic signal patterns solely from the observed dynamics, making this dataset an ideal benchmark for assessing the added interpretability of the proposed approach. By incorporating structural assumptions into the clustering process, K-Models enhances interpretability while maintaining performance comparable to state-of-the-art techniques, providing a valuable tool for analyzing functional data with an underlying ordinal structure.

</details>


### [212] [From Data to Action: Accelerating Refinery Optimization with AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15085)
*Dániel Pfeifer,Ábrahám Papp,Tibor Bernáth,Tamás Zoltán Varga,Márk Czifra,Botond Szilágyi,Edith Alice Kovács*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 针对炼油厂线性规划优化结果难以解读的问题，提出结合ECOD异常检测方法分析历史数据，通过选择高维数据中最具信息量的二维配对，识别业务机会和数据供应错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 炼油厂线性规划(LP)模型规模庞大（数十万矩阵元素），虽然数学上正确但存在模型简化和数据供应错误，且LP求解器无记忆能力，导致结果可信度不足，需要额外的洞察来验证和解释优化结果。

Method: 采用改进的ECOD异常检测方法，针对高维数据提出"选择最具有信息量的配对"策略进行维度约简，然后结合两种2D异常检测算法对LP输出结果进行分析。

Result: 在MOL炼油厂调度和规划架构中成功识别了多项业务机会和数据供应错误。

Conclusion: 异常检测方法与LP优化输出相结合，能够有效提升炼油厂优化结果的可解释性和可信度，帮助发现模型问题和实际业务价值。

Abstract: Nowadays refinery optimization utilizes sheer amounts of data, which can be handled with modern Linear Programming (LP) software, but the interpreting and applying the results remains challenging. Large petrochemical companies use massive models, with hundreds of thousands of input matrix elements. The LP solution is mathematically correct, but simplifications are made in the model, and data supply errors may occur. Therefore, further insight is needed to trust the results. The LP solver does not have a memory, so additional understanding could be gained by analyzing historical data and comparing it to the current plan.
