As such, machine learning approaches were suggested to support decision making based on the LP solution. Among these, Anomaly Detection tools are proposed to be used in tandem with the LP output. A transformed version of the popular ECOD methodology is applied. New methods are proposed to handle high-dimensional data: choosing the most informative pairs. Then, this is used alongside two 2D Anomaly Detection algorithms, revealing several business opportunities and data supply errors in the MOL refinery scheduling and planning architecture.

</details>


### [213] [Logging Policy Design for Off-Policy Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15108)
*Connor Douglas,Joel Persson,Foster Provost*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究离线策略评估（OPE）中日志策略的设计问题，揭示了奖励-覆盖率之间的权衡关系，并针对不同信息条件下推导出最优日志策略，为企业在多个候选推荐系统间选择时提供可操作的指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线策略评估（OPE）的准确性在很大程度上依赖于收集数据的日志策略，但如何设计日志策略以最小化OPE误差尚缺乏系统性的理论研究。

Method: 通过理论分析刻画了奖励-覆盖率之间的基本权衡，并在三种信息框架下（目标策略与奖励分布已知、未知、部分已知）推导出最优日志策略。

Result: 揭示了通过集中概率到高奖励动作可降低方差但会遗漏目标策略可能采取的动作信号这一核心权衡；给出了不同信息条件下的最优策略形式。

Conclusion: 为企业在收集OPE数据时的治疗选择提供了理论依据，并在操作约束下提炼出实用设计原则。

Abstract: Off-policy evaluation (OPE) estimates the value of a target treatment policy (e.g., a recommender system) using data collected by a different logging policy. It enables high-stakes experimentation without live deployment, yet in practice accuracy depends heavily on the logging policy used to collect data for computing the estimate. We study how to design logging policies that minimize OPE error for given target policies. We characterize a fundamental reward-coverage tradeoff: concentrating probability mass on high-reward actions reduces variance but risks missing signal on actions the target policy may take. We propose a unifying framework for logging policy design and derive optimal policies in canonical informational regimes where the target policy and reward distribution are (i) known, (ii) unknown, and (iii) partially known through priors or noisy estimates at logging time. Our results provide actionable guidance for firms choosing among multiple candidate recommendation systems. We demonstrate the importance of treatment selection when gathering data for OPE, and describe theoretically optimal approaches when this is a firm's primary objective. We also distill practical design principles for selecting logging policies when operational constraints prevent implementing the theoretical optimum.

</details>


### [214] [RoSHAP: A Distributional Framework and Robust Metric for Stable Feature Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15154)
*Lanxin Xiang,Liang Shi,Youhui Ye,Boyu Jiang,Dawei Zhou,Feng Guo*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出RoSHAP框架，通过自助重采样和核密度估计对SHAP特征归因分数的分布进行建模，得到渐近高斯分布，并据此构建一个同时考量活跃性、强度和稳定性的鲁棒特征排序指标。实验表明RoSHAP在识别信号特征和特征筛选后保持预测性能方面优于传统单次归因方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 特征归因分析在解释机器学习模型时至关重要，但现有特征归因度量存在随机波动：不同的训练-测试划分、随机种子或模型拟合过程会产生差异很大的归因值和特征排序。这削弱了模型解释的可靠性和一致性。

Method: 提出一个框架对特征归因得分的分布进行建模，通过自助重采样(Bootstrap)和核密度估计(Kernel Density Estimation)来估计该分布。在温和正则条件下证明了聚合后的特征归因得分渐近服从高斯分布，从而大幅降低分布估计的计算成本。基于此分布构建RoSHAP指标，将SHAP分布总结为一个同时奖励活跃性(active)、强度(strong)和稳定性(stable)的鲁棒特征排序准则。

Result: 在模拟实验和真实数据实验中，RoSHAP框架在识别信号特征方面优于标准单次归因度量。使用RoSHAP筛选的特征构建的模型在预测性能上与全特征模型相当，但使用的预测变量数量显著减少。

Conclusion: RoSHAP方法提高了机器学习模型的稳定性和可解释性，能够为分析提供可靠且一致的洞察，有助于支持可靠的数据驱动决策。

Abstract: Feature attribution analysis is critical for interpreting machine learning models and supporting reliable data-driven decisions. However, feature attribution measures often exhibit stochastic variation: different train--test splits, random seeds, or model-fitting procedures can produce substantially different attribution values and feature rankings. This paper proposes a framework for incorporating stochastic nature of feature attribution and a robust attribution metric, RoSHAP, for stable feature ranking based on the SHAP metric. The proposed framework models the distribution of feature attribution scores and estimates it through bootstrap resampling and kernel density estimation. We show that, under mild regularity conditions, the aggregated feature attribution score is asymptotically Gaussian, which greatly reduces the computational cost of distribution estimation. The RoSHAP summarizes the distribution of SHAP into a robust feature-ranking criterion that simultaneously rewards features that are active, strong, and stable. Through simulations and real-data experiments, the proposed framework and RoSHAP outperform standard single-run attribution measures in identifying signal features. In addition, models built using RoSHAP-selected features achieve predictive performance comparable to full-feature models while using substantially fewer predictors. The proposed RoSHAP approach improves the stability and interpretability of machine learning models, enabling reliable and consistent insights for analysis.

</details>


### [215] [AIS: Adaptive Importance Sampling for Quantized RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13907)
*Jiajun Zhou,Wei Shao,Lingchao Zheng,Yuwei Fan,Ngai Wong*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 针对LLM强化学习中低精度rollout（FP8）与BF16训练器不匹配导致的策略梯度偏差问题，提出自适应重要性采样（AIS）方法，通过三个实时诊断指标动态调整校正强度，保留探索收益同时抑制不稳定偏差，在保持1.5-2.76倍rollout加速的同时匹配BF16基线性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM强化学习受限于rollout生成的计算成本，低精度rollout（FP8）与BF16训练器之间的不匹配会导致策略梯度偏差，甚至使训练在推理任务上完全崩溃，需要解决这一非平稳的双刃剑效应。

Method: 提出自适应重要性采样（AIS），结合权重可靠性、散度严重性和方差放大三个实时诊断指标，动态生成混合系数，在未校正梯度与完全重要性加权梯度之间插值，抑制偏差的破坏性成分同时保留探索收益。

Result: 在LLaDA-8B-Instruct、Qwen3-8B和Qwen3.5-9B上，AIS在多数任务上匹配BF16基线性能，同时保持FP8带来的1.5-2.76倍rollout加速。

Conclusion: AIS有效解决了低精度rollout与高精度训练器不匹配问题，实现了在不牺牲性能的前提下大幅提升rollout效率。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) for large language models (LLMs) is dominated by the cost of rollout generation, which has motivated the use of low-precision rollouts (e.g., FP8) paired with a BF16 trainer to improve throughput and reduce memory pressure. This introduces a rollout-training mismatch that biases the policy gradient and can cause training to collapse outright on reasoning benchmarks. We show that the mismatch is non-stationary and acts as a double-edged sword: early in training it provides a stochastic exploration bonus, exposing the gradient to trajectories the trainer would otherwise under-sample, but the same perturbation transitions into a destabilizing source of bias as the policy concentrates.
  To solve this, we propose Adaptive Importance Sampling (AIS), a correction framework that adjusts the strength of its intervention on a per-batch basis. AIS combines three real-time diagnostics, namely weight reliability, divergence severity, and variance amplification, into a single mixing coefficient that interpolates between the uncorrected and fully importance-weighted gradients, suppressing the destabilizing component of the mismatch while preserving its exploratory benefit. We integrate AIS into GRPO and evaluate it on the diffusion-based LLaDA-8B-Instruct and the autoregressive Qwen3-8B and Qwen3.5-9B across mathematical reasoning and planning benchmarks. AIS matches the BF16 baseline on most tasks while retaining the 1.5 to 2.76x rollout speedup of FP8.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [216] [Double/debiased machine learning of quantile treatment effects on long-term outcomes in clinical trials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14275)
*Ziyang Liu,Niwen Zhou,Peng Wu,Xu Guo*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究通过结合随机试验数据与外部观察数据，在仅获得短期替代结局的情况下，估计试验人群长期结局的分位数处理效应，并提出了双重稳健的估计方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机临床试验中长期结局通常难以获得，而短期替代结局虽常见但信息有限；外部观察数据包含长期结局但存在混杂偏倚。现有方法多聚焦于平均处理效应，缺乏对分位数处理效应的研究。

Method: 在治疗随机化、正性假设和基于替代结局的可运输性假设下建立识别条件，提出双重稳健估计器；该估计器允许使用灵活的机器学习方法进行 nuisance 估计，且只要评分函数或结局回归相关的 nuisance 函数之一被一致估计，估计量即保持一致。

Result: 在正则性条件下，估计量具有渐近正态性；模拟和真实数据结果表明该方法在有限样本下表现良好，能够揭示不同分位数上异质性的长期处理效应。

Conclusion: 该方法有效填补了现有研究在长期结局分位数处理效应估计方面的空白，为整合随机试验与观察数据提供了稳健的推断工具。

Abstract: Long-term outcomes are often unavailable in randomized clinical trials, although short-term surrogate outcomes are commonly observed. External observational data may contain the long-term outcome, but causal comparisons based on such data alone are vulnerable to confounding. Existing surrogate-based data integration methods for long-term outcomes have focused primarily on average treatment effects. We study estimation of quantile treatment effects for long-term outcomes in the trial population by combining randomized trial data with external observational data. Under treatment randomization, positivity, and a surrogate-based transportability assumption, we establish identification and develop a doubly robust estimator for inference. The estimator accommodates flexible machine learning methods for nuisance estimation, remains consistent if either the score-related or outcome regression-related nuisance functions are consistently estimated, and is asymptotically normal under regularity conditions. Simulation and real-data results demonstrate that the proposed method performs well in finite samples and can reveal heterogeneous long-term treatment effects across quantiles.

</details>


### [217] [Asymptotic Anytime-Valid Inference for U-statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14692)
*Leheng Cai,Qirui Hu,Weijia Li*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了连续监测下二阶U-统计量的渐近任意时有效置信序列。在非退化情形下，基于Hoeffding投影和留一法Jackknife估计构建了数据驱动的置信序列，达到渐近覆盖保证；在退化情形下，开发了谱分配高斯混沌游走边界（SAGE），并基于截断谱估计实现插件法实施，两种情形分别达到$\sqrt{\log\log n/n}$和$\log\log n/n$的最优时间一致收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为连续监测下二阶U-统计量的在线推断提供严格的任意时有效置信序列理论，填补退化情形下序贯推断方法匮乏的空白。

Method: 非退化情形使用Hoeffding投影将问题转化为部分和的时间一致中心极限定理，并用留一法Jackknife估计方差；退化情形通过谱分配高斯混沌游走（SAGE）边界近似二次型高斯混沌过程，辅以截断谱估计实现插件法。

Result: 非退化情形置信区间宽度实现$\sqrt{\log\log n/n}$最优速率，退化情形实现$\log\log n/n$最优速率；数值实验验证了理论有效性。

Conclusion: 为连续监测下U-统计量的序贯推断提供了统一的渐近理论框架，尤其解决了退化情形下推断困难的核心问题，给出了可实现的最优速率边界。

Abstract: We study asymptotic anytime-valid confidence sequences for degree-two U-statistics under continuous monitoring. In the nondegenerate case, Hoeffding's projection reduces the problem to a time-uniform central limit theory for the partial sums of the first-order projection, while the canonical remainder is shown to be negligible under mild moment assumptions. A leave-one-out jackknife estimator then yields a fully data-driven procedure, leading to confidence sequences with asymptotic coverage guarantee for the parameter of interest. In the degenerate case, we show that the U-statistic is approximated by a centered quadratic Gaussian-chaos rather than by a simple Gaussian, which poses significant challenges for sequential inference. To address this issue, we novelly develop the Spectrally Allocated Gaussian-chaos Excursion (SAGE) boundary, and then provide plug-in implementations based on truncated spectrum estimation with consistency guarantees. The resulting widths can attain the expected time-uniform optimal rates: $\sqrt{\log\log n/n}$ in the nondegenerate regime and $\log\log n/n$ in the degenerate regime. Several widely used U-statistics are discussed within the proposed framework, and numerical experiments further support the validity of the derived theory.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [218] [Nearest-Neighbor Radii under Dependent Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14343)
*Yuanyuan Gao,Yilong Hou,Zhexiao Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了依赖采样下最近邻半径的几何性质，证明在强混合依赖观测下最近邻距离仍保持与独立采样相同的尺度，并建立了多项式混合下的几乎必然收敛和几何混合下的非渐近矩界，结果依赖于局部内在维度而非环境维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典和现代机器学习中的最近邻方法通常假设独立采样，但现实数据往往存在时间或空间上的依赖性。本文旨在探究依赖采样是否会改变最近邻邻域的尺度（即最近邻半径的大小），从而为依赖数据下的最近邻方法提供理论基础。

Method: 理论分析：在强混合（strong mixing）依赖观测假设下，研究最近邻半径。分别考虑多项式混合和几何混合两种依赖强度，建立分布自由的几乎必然收敛结果和非渐近矩界。实验验证：通过合成实验和真实世界时间序列基准数据支持理论结论。

Result: 依赖采样下最近邻几何仍保持信息量，与独立采样相似。在多项式混合条件下建立了分布自由的几乎必然收敛性；在几何混合条件下给出了依赖于局部内在维度（而非环境维度）的尖锐非渐近矩界，适用于高维数据集中低维流形的情形。

Conclusion: 依赖采样不会根本改变最近邻半径的尺度，最近邻几何在依赖数据下仍然有效和信息丰富。结果依赖于局部内在维度，适用于高维低维流形结构的数据。

Abstract: Nearest-neighbor methods are fundamental to classical and modern machine learning, yet their geometric properties are typically analyzed under independent sampling. In this paper, we study the nearest-neighbor radii under dependent sampling. We consider strong mixing dependent observations and ask whether dependence changes the scale of nearest-neighbor neighborhoods. We establish distribution-free almost sure convergence under polynomial mixing and sharp non-asymptotic moment bounds under geometric mixing. The moment bounds depend on the local intrinsic dimension rather than the ambient dimension, making the results applicable to high-dimensional data concentrated near lower-dimensional manifolds. Synthetic experiments and real-world time-series benchmarks support the theory, showing that nearest-neighbor geometry remains informative under dependence sampling.

</details>


### [219] [Vision-Based Runtime Monitoring under Varying Specifications using Semantic Latent Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13923)
*Bardh Hoxha,Oliver Schön,Hideki Okamoto,Lars Lindemann,Georgios Fainekos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在部分可观测条件下，基于视觉观测对过去时间信号时序逻辑（ptSTL）进行认证运行时监控。提出可重用监控器：通过语义基（语义基）和滚动预测监控器两种方法，无需针对每个公式重新训练即可认证目标片段内的任意公式，并在仿真和真实驾驶数据上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有监控方法通常需要针对每个公式单独训练或校准，缺乏可重用性。同时，从图像中推导安全相关量并提供有限样本保证是一个挑战。

Method: 1) 提出语义基（semantic basis）作为最小预测目标，即时间原子的鲁棒性得分向量，通过确定性解码器评估任意公式；2) 通过单次共形校准即可认证整个片段，无需联合界；3) 提出滚动预测监控器（rolling prediction monitor），仅预测当前谓词值并在线重构时序历史。

Result: 在行人交叉路口基准上，滚动监控器在短时间窗上认证界限更紧，语义基监控器在长时间窗上紧4倍。在真实Waymo驾驶数据上，两种监控器均满足共形覆盖保证。

Conclusion: 语义基方法在长时间窗上表现更优，滚动方法在短时间窗上更紧。两种方法均可重用且提供有限样本保证，适用于实际自动驾驶安全监控场景。

Abstract: We study certified runtime monitoring of past-time signal temporal logic (ptSTL) from visual observations under partial observability. The monitor must infer safety-relevant quantities from images and provide finite-sample guarantees, while being \emph{reusable}: once trained and calibrated, it should certify any formula in a target fragment without per-formula retraining. For fragments induced by a finite dictionary of temporal atoms, we prove that the \emph{semantic basis}, the vector of atom robustness scores, is the minimum prediction target within the class of monotone, 1-Lipschitz reusable interfaces: any formula is evaluated by a deterministic decoder derived from the parse tree, and a single conformal calibration pass certifies the entire fragment with no union bound. We also introduce a \emph{rolling prediction monitor} that predicts only current predicate values and reconstructs temporal history online; this is easier to learn but grows conservative at long horizons. On a pedestrian-crossroad benchmark, rolling achieves tighter certified bounds at short horizons while the semantic-basis monitor is up to 4-times tighter at long horizons. We validate the presented monitors on real-world Waymo driving data, where both monitors satisfy the conformal coverage guarantee empirically.

</details>


### [220] [Mechanistic Interpretability of EEG Foundation Models via Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13930)
*William Lehn-Schiøler,Magnus Ruud Kjær,Rahul Thapa,Magnus Guldberg Pedersen,Anton Storgaard Mosquera,Nick Williams,Radu Gatej,Tue Lehn-Schiøler,Sándor Beniczky,Sadasivan Puthusserypady,James Zou,Lars Kai Hansen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在三个不同架构的EEG transformer（SleepFM、REVE、LaBraM）上应用TopK稀疏自编码器提取稀疏特征字典，通过临床分类法（异常、年龄、性别、用药）基准测试了单义性和纠缠度。引入"目标vs脱靶"探测区域指标量化概念引导的选择性，揭示了三种操作状态：可选择性引导、编码但纠缠、未编码。发现了"破坏球"干预和临床纠缠（如年龄-病理混杂）等关键表征失败。最后，频谱解码器将潜在操作映射回振幅谱，转换为生理可解释的频率特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG基础模型在临床任务上表现优异，但其内部计算过程不透明，阻碍了临床信任。现有可解释性方法难以揭示模型如何编码和解码临床相关概念。

Method: 在三个不同架构的EEG transformer（SleepFM、REVE、LaBraM）上应用TopK稀疏自编码器（SAEs）提取稀疏特征字典；基于临床分类法（异常、年龄、性别、用药）进行单义性和纠缠度基准测试；提出"目标vs脱靶"探测区域指标量化概念引导选择性；使用频谱解码器将干预映射回振幅谱。

Result: 发现单一超参数程序可在三种架构间稳健迁移；揭示了三种操作状态：可选择性引导、编码但纠缠、未编码；发现"破坏球"干预和临床纠缠（如年龄-病理混杂）等关键表征失败；频谱解码器成功将潜在操作转换为生理可解释的频谱特征（如病理性慢波抑制和α波段恢复）。

Conclusion: 该框架系统性地揭示了EEG基础模型表征中的临床相关概念编码模式与失败案例，为模型调试和临床信任建立提供了可解释性工具，能够将潜在干预转化为生理可解释的频谱信号。

Abstract: EEG foundation models achieve state-of-the-art clinical performance, yet the internal computations driving their predictions remain opaque: a barrier to clinical trust. We apply TopK Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) across three architecturally distinct EEG transformers: SleepFM, REVE, and LaBraM to extract sparse feature dictionaries from their embeddings. By grounding these features in a clinical taxonomy (abnormality, age, sex, and medication), we benchmark monosemanticity and entanglement across architectures. A single hyperparameter procedure, driven by an intrinsic dictionary health audit, transfers robustly across all three architectures. Via concept steering, we introduce a "target vs. off-target" probe area metric to quantify steering selectivity and reveal three operational regimes: selectively steerable, encoded but entangled, and non-encoded. This framework exposes critical representational failures: "wrecking-ball" interventions that collapse global model performance, and clinical entanglements, such as age-pathology confounding, where it is impossible to suppress one concept without corrupting the other. Finally, a spectral decoder maps these interventions back to the amplitude spectrum, translating latent manipulations into physiologically interpretable frequency signatures, such as pathological slow-wave suppression and $α$-band restoration.

</details>


### [221] [Unsupervised learning of acquisition variability in structural connectomes via hybrid latent space modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13933)
*Gaurav Rudravaram,Lianrui Zuo,Karthik Ramadass,Elyssa McMaster,Jongyeon Yoon,Aravind R. Krishnan,Adam M. Saunders,Chenyu Gao,Nancy R. Newlin,Praitayini Kanakaraj,Lori L. Beason Held,Murat Bilgel,Laura A. Barquero,Micah DArchangel,Tin Q. Nguyen,Laurie B. Cutting,Derek Archer,Timothy J. Hohman,Daniel C. Moyer,Bennett A. Landman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需手动调参的无监督框架（Joint-VAE），通过架构退火（architectural annealing）在编码器输出与解码器之间平衡离散和连续潜变量，自动分离dMRI结构连接组中的采集效应（扫描仪/协议差异）与生物变异。在N=7,416（年龄2-102岁，13项研究，25种采集参数组合）的大规模数据集上，相比标准VAE、PCA+k-means及仅通过损失函数退火的混合模型，该方法在捕获采集变异性上取得显著提升（ARI=0.53, p<0.05）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统降维方法将所有变异建模为连续变量，导致采集效应（扫描仪/协议差异）被吸收进连续潜空间；现有混合潜空间模型虽引入离散+连续组分分离采集变异，但需手动调节容量（capacity tuning）才能使离散组分捕获目标变异，缺乏自动化机制。

Method: 提出Joint-VAE无监督框架，核心创新是架构退火（architectural annealing）：在编码器输出后、解码器前通过架构设计逐步调整离散和连续潜变量的平衡，无需手动调参。在7,416个dMRI结构连接组上，与标准VAE、PCA+k-means、仅损失退火的混合模型进行对比。

Result: 架构退火方法在捕获站点/扫描仪差异上显著优于基线（ARI=0.53, p<0.05）；联合连续-离散潜空间能自动恢复与扫描仪和协议差异对齐的聚类。

Conclusion: 联合建模平滑与类别结构的混合潜空间，配合架构级而非损失级的退火策略，为dMRI中捕获采集变异性提供了一种有效的无监督机制。

Abstract: Acquisition differences across sites, scanners, and protocols in dMRI introduce variability that complicates structural connectome analysis. This motivates deep learning models that can represent high-dimensional connectomes in a low-dimensional space while explicitly separating acquisition-related effects from biological variation. Conventional dimensionality reduction methods model all variance as continuous, so acquisition effects often get absorbed into a continuous latent space. Recent hybrid latent-space models combine discrete and continuous components to address this, but typically require manual capacity tuning to ensure the discrete component captures the intended variability. We introduce an unsupervised framework that removes this manual tuning by architecturally annealing encoder outputs before decoding, allowing the model to adaptively balance discrete and continuous latent variables during training. To evaluate it, we curated a dataset of N=7,416 structural connectomes derived from dMRI, spanning ages 2 to 102 and 13 studies with 25 unique acquisition-parameter combinations. Of these, 5,900 are cognitively unimpaired, 877 have mild cognitive impairment (MCI), and 639 have Alzheimer's disease (AD). We compare against a standard VAE, PCA with k-means clustering, and hybrid models that anneal only through the loss function. Our architectural annealing produces stronger site learning (ARI=0.53, p<0.05) than these baselines. Results show that a hybrid continuous-discrete latent space, with architectural rather than loss-based annealing, provides a useful unsupervised mechanism for capturing acquisition variability in dMRI: by jointly modeling smooth and categorical structure, the Joint-VAE recovers clusters aligned with scanner and protocol differences.

</details>


### [222] [Beyond Mode-Seeking RL: Trajectory-Balance Post-Training for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13935)
*Saba Ahmadi,Prasanna Parthasarathi,Yufei Cui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对扩散语言模型后训练中奖励驱动更新导致"轨迹锁定"（概率过度集中于少数去噪路径）的问题，提出TraFL方法，通过轨迹平衡目标将策略训练向奖励倾斜的目标分布，在数学推理和代码生成基准上唯一在所有评估设置中优于基础模型的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散语言模型的后训练方法主要采用奖励最大化目标，这导致了一个关键失效模式——轨迹锁定：采样奖励驱动更新将概率质量过度集中于狭窄的去噪路径集合，减少了重复采样下正确解的覆盖范围。

Method: 提出TraFL（Trajectory Flow baLancing），采用轨迹平衡目标，将策略训练导向一个以冻结参考模型为锚定的奖励倾斜目标分布；并提出扩散兼容的序列级替代方法和学习得到的提示相关归一化因子使其适用于扩散语言模型。

Result: 在数学推理和代码生成基准上，TraFL是唯一在所有基准长度设置下均优于基础模型的评估后训练方法，且增益随采样预算增加而持续。改进可迁移至未见评估：在Minerva Math上保持高于基础模型，在LiveCodeBench的所有难度划分上均为最强方法。

Conclusion: TraFL有效解决了扩散语言模型后训练中的轨迹锁定问题，通过轨迹平衡目标实现了更稳定的性能提升和更好的解覆盖。

Abstract: Diffusion language models are a promising alternative to autoregressive models, yet post-training methods for them largely adapt reward-maximizing objectives. We identify a central failure mode in this setting we call trajectory locking: sampled reward-driven updates over-concentrate probability mass onto a narrow set of denoising paths, reducing coverage of alternative correct solutions under repeated sampling. To address this, we propose TraFL (Trajectory Flow baLancing), a trajectory-balance objective that trains the policy toward a reward-tilted target distribution anchored to a frozen reference model. We make this practical for diffusion language models with a diffusion-compatible sequence-level surrogate and a learned prompt-dependent normalization. Across mathematical reasoning and code generation benchmarks, TraFL is the only evaluated post-training method that improves over the base model in every benchmark-length setting, with gains that persist as the sampling budget increases. The improvements transfer to held-out evaluations: TraFL stays above the base model on Minerva Math and is the strongest method on every LiveCodeBench difficulty split.

</details>


### [223] [Towards the Next Frontier of LLMs, Training on Private Data: A Cross-Domain Benchmark for Federated Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13936)
*Daniel M. Jimenez-Gutierrez,Enrique Zuazua,Georgios Kellaris,Joaquin del Rio,Oleksii Sliusarenko,Xabi Uribe-Etxebarria*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于联邦学习的框架，使多个机构能在不共享隐私数据的情况下联合微调大语言模型，跨医疗和金融领域的实验证明联邦微调接近集中训练效果且优于单机构学习，其中QLoRA和IA3等参数高效微调策略在绿色AI方面表现更佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的成功依赖大量公共数据，但最有价值的信息分布在医疗机构和金融机构等私有数据孤岛中，受隐私、法规和组织壁垒限制无法共享，且这些数据是非独立同分布的（non-IID），阻碍了LLM在专业领域的深度应用。

Method: 基于Sherpa.ai联邦学习平台构建框架，使各节点在不交换原始数据的情况下联合微调共享LLM；采用LoRA、QLoRA和IA3三种参数高效微调策略；在医疗（MedQA、MedMCQA）和金融（FPB、FiQA-SA）四个数据集上进行跨领域评估，模拟non-IID的机构数据异质性场景。

Result: 联邦微调性能接近集中式训练，优于单机构独立学习；在绿色AI视角下，QLoRA和IA3以有限的准确率下降显著提升了效率。

Conclusion: 联邦参数高效微调（Federated PEFT）是在数据无法共享场景下适配LLM的可行方案，能够解锁分布式私有数据价值，推动LLM向更深度的领域专业知识发展。

Abstract: The recent success of large language models (LLMs) has been largely driven by vast public datasets. However, the next frontier for LLM development lies beyond public data. Much of the world's most valuable information is private, especially in highly regulated sectors such as healthcare and finance, where data include patient histories or customer communications. Unlocking this data could represent a major leap forward, enabling LLMs with deeper domain expertise and stronger real-world utility. Yet, these data cannot be shared because they are distributed across institutions and constrained by privacy, regulatory, and organizational barriers. Moreover, institutional datasets are typically non-independent and identically distributed (non-IID), differing across sites in population characteristics, data modalities, documentation patterns, and task-specific label distributions.
  In this paper, we demonstrate a practical approach to unlocking private and distributed institutional data for LLM adaptation through federated collaboration across data silos. Built on the Sherpa.ai Federated Learning platform, our framework enables nodes to jointly fine-tune a shared LLM without exchanging private data. We evaluate this approach through a cross-domain benchmark in healthcare and finance, using four closed-ended question answering and classification datasets: MedQA, MedMCQA, FPB, and FiQA-SA. We compare three parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) strategies-LoRA, QLoRA, and IA3-across pretrained backbones under non-IID settings reflecting institutional data heterogeneity. Our results show that federated fine-tuning performs close to centralized training and outperforms isolated single-institution learning. From a Green AI perspective, QLoRA and IA3 improve efficiency with limited accuracy degradation, supporting federated PEFT as a viable approach for adapting LLMs where data cannot be shared.

</details>


### [224] [EvolveMem:Self-Evolving Memory Architecture via AutoResearch for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13941)
*Jiaqi Liu,Xinyu Ye,Peng Xia,Zeyu Zheng,Cihang Xie,Mingyu Ding,Huaxiu Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EvolveMem提出了一种自进化的记忆架构，不仅更新存储内容，还通过LLM驱动的诊断模块动态优化检索配置（如评分函数、融合策略等），实现记忆系统的完全自适应进化。在LoCoMo和MemBench上分别超越最强基线25.7%和18.9%，且进化策略可跨基准迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆系统的检索基础设施是固定的——存储内容可更新，但评分函数、融合策略和答案生成策略在部署后冻结。为了实现真正的自适应记忆，需要存储知识和检索机制在两个层面上协同进化。

Method: EvolveMem采用闭环自进化机制：LLM驱动的诊断模块读取每问题失败日志，识别根因，提出配置调整方案；受保护的元分析器在应用调整时包含自动回归回滚和停滞探索保护机制，实现AutoResearch过程——系统自主对其架构进行迭代研究。

Result: 在LoCoMo上，EvolveMem超越最强基线25.7%，相比最低基线提升78.0%。在MemBench上，超越最强基线18.9%。进化配置跨基准呈正迁移，表明捕获了通用检索原则而非基准特定启发式。

Conclusion: 自进化记忆架构能自动发现有效的检索策略，包括原始动作空间中不存在的新配置维度，且进化结果具有良好泛化性，验证了记忆系统双层面协同进化的有效性。

Abstract: Long-term memory is essential for LLM agents that operate across multiple sessions, yet existing memory systems treat retrieval infrastructure as fixed: stored content evolves while scoring functions, fusion strategies, and answer-generation policies remain frozen at deployment. We argue that truly adaptive memory requires co-evolution at two levels: the stored knowledge and the retrieval mechanism that queries it. We present EvolveMem, a self-evolving memory architecture that exposes its full retrieval configuration as a structured action space optimized by an LLM-powered diagnosis module. In each evolution round, the module reads per-question failure logs, identifies root causes, and proposes targeted configuration adjustments; a guarded meta-analyzer applies them with automatic revert-on-regression and explore-on-stagnation safeguards. This closed-loop self-evolution realizes an AutoResearch process: the system autonomously conducts iterative research cycles on its own architecture, replacing manual configuration tuning. Starting from a minimal baseline, the process converges autonomously, discovering effective retrieval strategies including entirely new configuration dimensions not present in the original action space. On LoCoMo, EvolveMem outperforms the strongest baseline by 25.7% relative and achieves a 78.0% relative improvement over the minimal baseline. On MemBench, EvolveMem exceeds the strongest baseline by 18.9% relative. Evolved configurations transfer across benchmarks with positive rather than catastrophic transfer, indicating that the self-evolution process captures universal retrieval principles rather than benchmark-specific heuristics. Code is available at https://github.com/aiming-lab/SimpleMem.

</details>


### [225] [A Unified Geometric Framework for Weighted Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13943)
*Raphael Vock,Edouard Duchesnay,Benoit Dufumier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明加权InfoNCE目标函数可解释为距离几何问题，揭示了对比学习中相似度权重与嵌入几何之间的精确关系：平衡监督对比学习产生经典正则单形体几何，类别不平衡导致非均匀类间相似度；Soft SupCon无论类别是否平衡都保持正则单形体；连续标签设置下，y-Aware CL存在标签权重与潜在相似度的几何不匹配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对比学习旨在通过表征保留样本间的关系结构，但所得嵌入的几何特性尚未被充分理解。本文旨在建立加权InfoNCE目标与距离几何问题之间的联系，从而刻画最优嵌入的几何结构。

Method: 将加权InfoNCE目标函数重新解释为距离几何问题（Distance Geometry Problems），其中权重方案指定了表征需要实现的目标几何结构。通过理论分析导出多种监督和弱监督设置下的最优嵌入的精确刻画。

Result: 1) 监督分类中，Balanced SupCon恢复经典正则单形体几何，类别不平衡导致非均匀类间相似度；2) Soft SupCon无论类别平衡与否都保持正则单形体；3) y-Aware CL在连续标签设置下存在几何不匹配，其最优解要求标签位于超球面上；4) 几何一致的权重选择（如欧几里得-欧几里得加权或X-CLR）存在唯一最优嵌入。

Conclusion: 对比学习中权重方案的选择决定了学习目标在几何上是否可实现、是否退化或是否不一致，为设计对比学习目标函数提供了原则性框架。

Abstract: Contrastive learning (CL) aims to preserve relational structure between samples by learning representations that reflect a similarity graph. Yet, the geometry of the resulting embeddings remains poorly understood. Here we show that weighted InfoNCE objectives can be interpreted as Distance Geometry Problems, where the weighting scheme specifies the target geometry to be realized by the representation. This viewpoint yields exact characterizations of the optimal embeddings for several supervised and weakly supervised objectives. In supervised classification, both SupCon and Soft SupCon (a dense relaxation of it where pairs from distinct classes have small non-zero similarity) collapse samples within each class to a single prototype. However, while balanced SupCon recovers the classical regular simplex geometry, class imbalance breaks this symmetry: SupCon induces non-uniform inter-class similarities depending on class sizes, whereas Soft SupCon preserves a regular simplex geometry regardless of class imbalance. In continuous-label settings, our framework reveals a different failure mode: y-Aware CL generally cannot attain its entropic optimum unless the labels lie on a hypersphere, exposing a mismatch between Euclidean label weights and spherical latent similarity. By contrast, geometrically consistent choices such as Euclidean-Euclidean weighting or X-CLR admit unique optimal embeddings. Our results show that the choice of weighting scheme determines whether contrastive learning is geometrically realizable, degenerate, or inconsistent, providing a principled framework for designing contrastive objectives.

</details>


### [226] [Collider-Bench: Benchmarking AI Agents with Particle Physics Analysis Reproduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13950)
*Darius A. Faroughy,Sofia Palacios Schweitzer,Ian Pang,Siddharth Mishra-Sharma,David Shih*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Collider-Bench基准测试，用于评估LLM智能体在大型强子对撞机（LHC）实验分析复现任务上的表现，要求智能体仅利用公开论文和开源科学软件，将已发表的分析转化为可执行的模拟与筛选流水线，并提交指定信号区域中的碰撞事件产额预测。实验表明，平均而言，没有任何通用编码智能体可靠地超越物理学家参与求解的方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试难以捕捉真实科学工作的复杂性和细微差别，特别是缺乏对自主语言模型智能体在长周期工具使用任务（如复现LHC实验分析）上的评估能力。

Method: 构建Collider-Bench基准，包含LHC搜索任务、容器化沙箱和事件模拟工具；要求智能体将已发表分析转化为可执行模拟-筛选流水线；使用标准直方图指标评估预测产额；通过LLM裁判检测代码和会话轨迹中的伪造、幻觉和重复等失败模式；报告每个智能体每项任务的计算成本。

Result: 平均而言，没有任何通用编码智能体可靠地超越物理学家参与求解（physicist-in-the-loop）的方案。

Conclusion: 当前通用编码智能体在复杂科学工作流复现任务上仍难以匹敌领域专家参与的求解方式，Collider-Bench为未来提升LLM智能体科学推理与工具使用能力提供了有价值的评估平台。

Abstract: Autonomous language-model agents are increasingly evaluated on long-horizon tool-use tasks, but existing benchmarks rarely capture the complexity and nuance of real scientific work. To address this gap, we introduce Collider-Bench, a benchmark for evaluating whether LLM agents can reproduce experimental analyses from the Large Hadron Collider (LHC) using only public papers and open scientific software. Such analyses are often difficult to reproduce because the public toolchain only approximates the software used internally by the experimental collaborations, while the published papers inevitably omit implementation details needed for a faithful reconstruction. Agents must therefore rely on physical reasoning, domain knowledge, and trial-and-error to fill these gaps. Each task requires the agent to turn a published analysis into an executable simulation-and-selection pipeline and submit predicted collision event yields in specified signal regions. These predictions are evaluated with standard histogram metrics that provide continuous fidelity scores without a hand-written rubric. We also report the computational cost incurred by each agent per task. Finally, we evaluate the codebase and full session trace using an LLM judge to catch qualitative failure modes such as fabrications, hallucinations and duplications. We release an initial set of tasks drawn from LHC searches, together with a containerized sandbox and event simulation tools. We evaluate across a capability ladder of general purpose coding agents. Our results show that on average no agent reliably beats the physicist-in-the-loop solution.

</details>


### [227] [WarmPrior: Straightening Flow-Matching Policies with Temporal Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13959)
*Sinjae Kang,Chanyoung Kim,Kaixin Wang,Li Zhao,Kimin Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出WarmPrior——一种基于近期动作历史构建的简单时间感知源分布，替代标准高斯分布用于扩散/流匹配的机器人视觉运动控制，在操作任务中持续提升成功率，并改善强化学习中的探索分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于扩散和流匹配的生成式策略已成为机器人视觉运动控制的主流范式，但源分布（通常为标准高斯分布）作为设计轴未被充分探索。作者试图探究通过引入具有时间结构的先验（近期动作历史）能否改善生成轨迹的概率路径，从而提升机器人操控任务的成功率。

Method: 将标准高斯源分布替换为WarmPrior——一种利用近期可获取的动作历史构建的简单时间接地先验。该方法可应用于行为克隆和基于先验空间的强化学习，通过更直的路径（类似Rectified Flow中的最优传输耦合效应）提升生成质量。

Result: WarmPrior在机器人操控任务上持续提升成功率；生成概率路径显著更直（echo最优传输耦合效应）；在强化学习场景中改善了探索分布，同时提升样本效率和最终性能。

Conclusion: 源分布是生成式机器人控制中重要但被低估的设计维度，WarmPrior作为一种简单有效的时间接地先验，为改进生成式策略提供了新方向。

Abstract: Generative policies based on diffusion and flow matching have become a dominant paradigm for visuomotor robotic control. We show that replacing the standard Gaussian source distribution with WarmPrior, a simple temporally grounded prior constructed from readily available recent action history, consistently improves success rates on robotic manipulation tasks. We trace this gain to markedly straighter probability paths, echoing the effect of optimal-transport couplings in Rectified Flow. Beyond standard behavior cloning, WarmPrior also reshapes the exploration distribution in prior-space reinforcement learning, improving both sample efficiency and final performance. Collectively, these results identify the source distribution as an important and underexplored design axis in generative robot control.

</details>


### [228] [Towards Resource-Efficient LLMs: End-to-End Energy Accounting of Distillation Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13981)
*Katherine Lambert,Sasha Luccioni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个全面的能耗核算框架，用于测量蒸馏管道的完整计算成本，包括教师侧的数据生成、logit缓存和评估等被忽略的环节。通过实验追踪两种常见蒸馏方法（基于logit的知识蒸馏和基于合成数据的监督微调）的阶段级GPU能耗，构建能量-质量帕累托前沿，并给出能耗约束下的方法选择指南，同时开源了标准化测量工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究在推广蒸馏技术时通常只声称其能获得更便宜、更高效的模型，但很少核算完整的端到端能耗和资源成本，特别是教师侧工作负载（数据生成、logit缓存、评估）被普遍忽略。作者旨在揭示这些被隐藏的成本，并提供可量化的分析框架。

Method: 提出一个全面的能耗核算框架，通过详细的阶段级GPU设备功耗追踪来测量蒸馏管道的完整计算成本。实验中将能耗分阶段分离并记录，系统测量了两种常见蒸馏方法（经典logit知识蒸馏和合成数据监督微调）的能耗和排放，构建了能量-质量帕累托前沿。

Result: 生成了能量-质量帕累托前沿，揭示了之前被忽略的教师侧能耗成本，并基于测量和分析得出了在能耗和预算约束下选择蒸馏方法和超参数的实用设计规则。

Conclusion: 开源了测量工具和核算协议，为可比较、可复现的蒸馏研究提供了标准化基础，使管道完整能耗影响能够被明确核算。

Abstract: The rise in deployment of large language models has driven a surge in GPU demand and datacenter scaling, raising concerns about electricity use, grid stress, and the impacts of modern AI workloads. Distillation is often promoted as one of the most effective paths to obtain cheaper, more efficient models, yet these claims rarely account for the full end-to-end energy and resource costs, including crucial teacher-side workloads such as data generation, logit caching, and evaluation. We present a comprehensive energy accounting framework that measures the complete computational cost of distillation pipelines via detailed stage-wise tracking of GPU device power consumption. In our experiments, we separate and log empirical energy use across distinct phases and systematically measure the energy and emissions of two common distillation methods: the classic logit-based knowledge distillation and synthetic-data supervised fine-tuning, constructing energy-quality Pareto frontiers that expose the previously ignored costs. From these measurements and analyses, we derive practical design rules for selecting distillation methods and hyperparameters under energy and budget constraints, and release an open-source measurement harness and accounting protocol to provide a standardized foundation for comparable, reproducible distillation research, explicitly accountable for complete pipeline energy impact.

</details>


### [229] [HodgeCover: Higher-Order Topological Coverage Drives Compression of Sparse Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13997)
*Tao Zhong,Dongzhe Zheng,Christine Allen-Blanchette*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现稀疏MoE层中三个专家两两兼容却可能形成不可约三元环，现有压缩方法因仅依赖成对信号而忽略此结构盲点。作者证明该障碍是2-复形上单纯拉普拉斯算子的调和核，提出HodgeCover方法通过覆盖调和关键边和三元关键三角形实现无损压缩，在激进压缩场景下达到最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习无关的MoE压缩方法仅考虑专家间的成对兼容性，忽略了三专家可能形成不可约三元环的结构性障碍，导致压缩效果受限。

Method: 将专家视为顶点、KL散度屏障视为边、三元组屏障视为面，构建2-复形；通过Hodge分解将边屏障信号分离出调和核成分；提出HodgeCover算法贪心覆盖调和关键边和三元关键三角形，并设计混合变体结合权重剪枝。

Result: 在三个开源稀疏MoE模型上，HodgeCover在专家缩减轴持平SOTA，在激进压缩的混合轴领先，且独特地实现了所有四个Hodge分量的质量保留平衡。

Conclusion: 揭示MoE结构的调和核改变了压缩器在最关键压缩场景下的胜负格局，说明考虑高阶拓扑结构对学习无关压缩至关重要。

Abstract: Sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) layers route tokens through a handful of experts, and learning-free compression of these layers reduces inference cost without retraining. A subtle obstruction blocks every existing compressor in this family: three experts can each be pairwise compatible yet form an irreducible cycle when merged together, so any score that ranks experts on pairwise signals is structurally blind to which triples are jointly mergeable. We show the obstruction is a precise mathematical object, the harmonic kernel of the simplicial Laplacian on a 2-complex whose vertices are experts, whose edges carry KL merge barriers, and whose faces carry triplet barriers; Hodge-decomposing the edge-barrier signal isolates the kernel exactly. We turn the diagnostic into a selection objective: HodgeCover greedily covers the harmonic-critical edges and triplet-critical triangles, and a hybrid variant of HodgeCover pairs it with off-the-shelf weight pruning on survivors. On three open-weight Sparse MoE backbones under aggressive expert reduction, HodgeCover matches state-of-the-art learning-free baselines on the expert-reduction axis, leads on the aggressive-compression frontier of the hybrid axis, and uniquely balances retained mass across all four Hodge components. These results show that exposing the harmonic kernel of a learned MoE structure changes which compressor wins at the regime that matters most.

</details>


### [230] [Support Before Frequency in Discrete Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13999)
*Adrian Müller,Antoine Gonon,Zebang Shen,Ya-Ping Hsieh,Niao He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 离散扩散模型在语言建模中日益具有竞争力，但其去噪目标如何组织学习仍不清楚。本文证明，精确反向过程在粗粒度支持信息与细粒度频率信息之间建立层级结构。对于均匀扩散和吸收（掩码）扩散，在最终去噪步骤的小噪声区域内，每个单token反向编辑分解为主导尺度（决定是否向数据支持移动，如语法有效句子）和精细系数（决定同一尺度内的相对概率）。因此，恢复有效性结构只需学习反向概率的数量级，而恢复数据频率需要系数级估计。这种分离是机制依赖的。实验支持了这些预测：支持定位早于支持内频率排序，均匀扩散与吸收扩散的对比符合预测的速率分离。总之，离散扩散模型先学习数据支持，后学习数据频率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解离散扩散模型去噪目标如何组织学习过程，阐明模型在训练中如何层次化地捕获数据结构。

Method: 理论分析均匀扩散和吸收扩散在小噪声区域的反向过程，证明单token反向编辑分解为支持尺度和频率系数；在masked语言扩散模型和合成正则语言任务上进行实验验证。

Result: 支持定位（学习数据支持结构）早于支持内频率排序（学习细粒度概率分布）；均匀扩散与吸收扩散的速率分离与理论预测一致。

Conclusion: 离散扩散模型的学习呈现层级性：先捕获数据支持（如语法有效性），再学习支持内的细粒度频率信息。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion models are increasingly competitive for language modeling, yet it remains unclear how their denoising objectives organize learning. Although these objectives target the full data distribution, we show that the exact reverse process induces a hierarchy between coarse support information and finer frequency information. For uniform and absorbing (a.k.a. masking) diffusion, we prove that, in the small-noise regime of the final denoising steps, each single-token reverse edit decomposes into a leading scale, determined by whether it moves toward the data support (e.g., grammatically valid sentences), and a finer coefficient, determining relative probabilities within the same scale. Thus, recovering validity structure only requires learning the correct order of magnitude of reverse probabilities, whereas recovering data frequencies requires coefficient-level estimation. The separation is mechanism-dependent: uniform diffusion exhibits a trichotomy into validity-improving, validity-preserving, and validity-worsening edits, while absorbing diffusion places its leading-order mass on validity-improving moves. Experiments on a masked language diffusion model and synthetic regular-language tasks support these predictions: support-localization emerges earlier than within-support frequency ranking, and the contrast between uniform and absorbing diffusion matches the predicted rate separation. Together, our results suggest that discrete diffusion models learn data support before data frequencies.

</details>


### [231] [Dywave: Event-Aligned Dynamic Tokenization for Heterogeneous IoT Sensing Signal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14014)
*Tomoyoshi Kimura,Denizhan Kara,Jinyang Li,Hongjue Zhao,Yigong Hu,Yizhuo Chen,Xiaomin Ouyang,Shengzhong Liu,Tarek Abdelzaher*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Dywave，一种面向物联网感知信号的动态分词框架，利用小波分层分解识别语义事件边界，自适应压缩冗余区间。在五个真实数据集上，相较SOTA方法准确率提升最高12%，输入长度减少最高75%，并展现出更强的领域迁移鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物联网系统持续采集的异构感知信号具有非平稳、多尺度的特性，标准分词技术难以有效处理这类信号，因此需要一种能够对齐内在时间结构与物理事件的紧凑输入表示方法。

Method: Dywave基于小波变换进行层次化分解，识别与底层语义事件对应的有意义时间边界，并自适应压缩冗余区间，同时保持时间连贯性，从而构建动态分词框架。

Result: 在活动识别、压力评估、近场物体检测五个真实数据集上，准确率比SOTA方法提升最高12%，主流序列模型输入token长度减少最高75%，且对领域偏移和变长序列具有更好的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: Dywave通过动态分词有效提升了IoT感知信号的处理效率与准确性，验证了小波分解在非平稳多尺度信号建模中的优势。

Abstract: Internet of Things (IoT) systems continuously collect heterogeneous sensing signals from ubiquitous sensors to support intelligent applications such as human activity analysis, emotion monitoring, and environmental perception. These signals are inherently non-stationary and multi-scale, posing unique challenges for standard tokenization techniques. This paper proposes Dywave, a dynamic tokenization framework for IoT sensing signals that constructs compact input representations aligned with intrinsic temporal structures and underlying physical events. Dywave leverages wavelet-based hierarchical decomposition, identifies meaningful temporal boundaries corresponding to underlying semantic events, and adaptively compresses redundant intervals while preserving temporal coherence. Extensive evaluations on five real-world IoT sensing datasets across activity recognition, stress assessment, and nearby object detection demonstrate that Dywave outperforms state-of-the-art methods by up to 12% in accuracy, while improving computational efficiency by reducing input token lengths by up to 75% across mainstream sequence models. Moreover, Dywave exhibits improved robustness to domain shifts and varying sequence lengths.

</details>


### [232] [R2R2: Robust Representation for Intensive Experience Reuse via Redundancy Reduction in Self-Predictive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14026)
*Sanghyeob Song,Donghyeok Lee,Jinsik Kim,Sungroh Yoon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对高更新-数据比(UTD)下自预测学习(SPL)中的表征级过拟合问题，提出R2R2正则化方法，通过非中心化冗余消除目标改善表征鲁棒性，在11个连续控制任务上验证有效性，TD7提升约22%，并扩展至SimbaV2-SPL实现新SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习在数据稀缺场景下高频数据复用会导致过拟合，先前工作主要关注评论家偏差问题，而自预测学习在高UTD比率下的表征级不稳定性尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出R2R2（Robust Representation via Redundancy Reduction），一种应用于自预测学习中的正则化方法。理论识别标准零中心化与SPL谱特性存在冲突，据此设计非中心化目标。在TD7等SPL原生算法上验证，并扩展至原无SPL的SimbaV2，集成定制SPL模块形成SimbaV2-SPL。

Result: 在11个连续控制任务上，UTD=20时TD7提升约22%，SimbaV2-SPL取得新的SOTA结果，R2R2在SimbaV2-SPL上提供额外增益，验证了其与先前进展的正交性。

Conclusion: R2R2有效缓解了高数据复用下的表征过拟合问题，且与现有方法正交互补，代码已开源。

Abstract: For reinforcement learning in data-scarce domains like real-world robotics, intensive data reuse enhances efficiency but induces overfitting. While prior works focus on critic bias, representation-level instability in Self-Predictive Learning (SPL) under high Update-to-Data (UTD) regimes remains underexplored. To bridge this gap, we propose Robust Representation via Redundancy Reduction (R2R2), a regularization method within SPL. We theoretically identify that standard zero-centering conflicts with SPL's spectral properties and design a non-centered objective accordingly. We verify R2R2 on SPL-native algorithms like TD7. Furthermore, to demonstrate its orthogonality to prior advancements, we extend the state-of-the-art SimbaV2, which originally lacks SPL, by integrating a tailored SPL module, termed SimbaV2-SPL. Experiments across 11 continuous control tasks confirm that R2R2 effectively mitigates overfitting; specifically, at a UTD ratio of 20, it improves TD7 by ~22% and provides additional gains on top of SimbaV2-SPL, which itself establishes a new state-of-the-art. The code can be found at: https://github.com/songsang7/R2R2

</details>


### [233] [Self-Pruned Key-Value Attention: Learning When to Write by Predicting Future Utility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14037)
*Gergely Szilvasy,Manuel Faysse,Maria Lomeli,Matthijs Douze,Pierre-Emmanuel Mazaré,Loïc Cabannes,Wen-tau Yih,Hervé Jégou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Self-Pruned Key-Value Attention (SP-KV)，一种通过轻量级效用预测器动态剪枝KV缓存的方法，在保持性能的同时将缓存大小减少3-10倍，并揭示了可用于指导混合注意力架构设计的结构化稀疏模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代测试时计算和智能体范式下，语言模型处理序列越来越长，但Transformer文本生成效率受限于KV缓存的内存占用和带宽瓶颈。

Method: SP-KV包含一个轻量级效用预测器，对每个键值对进行评分；近期KV通过局部窗口始终可用，而旧KV只有在预测效用超过阈值时才写入缓存并用于全局注意力。模型与预测器通过端到端下一个token预测损失联合训练，从预训练检查点微调而来。

Result: 动态稀疏化使KV缓存大小减少3-10倍，长序列压缩比更高；显著提升内存使用和解码速度，在验证损失和下游任务上几乎没有退化。

Conclusion: SP-KV不仅是一种有效的KV缓存压缩机制，还揭示了结构化层特异性和头特异性的稀疏模式，可指导混合局部-全局注意力架构的设计。

Abstract: Under modern test-time compute and agentic paradigms, language models process ever-longer sequences. Efficient text generation with transformer architectures is increasingly constrained by the Key-Value cache memory footprint and bandwidth. To address this limitation, we introduce Self-Pruned Key-Value Attention (SP-KV), a mechanism designed to predict future KV utility in order to reduce the size of the long-term KV cache. This strategy operates at a fine granularity: a lightweight utility predictor scores each key-value pair, and while recent KVs are always available via a local window, older pairs are written in the cache and used in global attention only if their predicted utility surpasses a given threshold. The LLM and the utility predictor are trained jointly end-to-end exclusively through next-token prediction loss, and are adapted from pretrained LLM checkpoints.
  Rather than enforcing a fixed compression ratio, SP-KV performs dynamic sparsification: the mechanism adapts to the input and typically reduces the KV cache size by a factor of $3$ to $10\times$, longer sequences often being more compressible. This leads to vast improvements in memory usage and decoding speed, with little to no degradation of validation loss nor performance on a broad set of downstream tasks. Beyond serving as an effective KV-cache reduction mechanism, our method reveals structured layer- and head-specific sparsity patterns that we can use to guide the design of hybrid local-global attention architectures.

</details>


### [234] [Reliability-Gated Source Anchoring for Continual Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14063)
*Vikash Singh,Debargha Ganguly,Weicong Chen,Sabyasachi Sahoo,Sreehari Sankar,Biyao Zhang,Mohsen Harir,Shouren Wang,Osama Zafar,Christian Gagné,Vipin Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RMemSafe方法，通过检测源模型预测熵来动态关闭源锚定，解决连续测试时自适应（CTTA）中源模型失效时的盲目锚定问题，在多个基准上显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有源锚定CTTA方法在源模型严重退化时（如ResNet-50在CCC-Hard上准确率降至约1.3%）仍保持相同锚定强度，导致"盲目锚定"失败模式，无法自适应地调整对退化源模型的依赖。

Method: RMemSafe是ROID的可信门控扩展，利用冻结源模型的归一化预测熵来衰减所有显式源耦合项：当源后验接近均匀分布时，门控关闭，源锚定和一致性滤波消失，目标函数退化为源无关的备用损失（ROID基础损失+边际校准）。

Result: 结合ASR后，RMemSafe在9个匹配分割持续污染单元中的8个取得最低错误率，在所有9个单元中是最佳基于重置的方法；相比ROID+ASR，在ResNet-50上提升1.05个百分点，在ViT-B/16上提升0.48个百分点；源退化扫描显示伤害斜率比ROID+ASR浅1.13倍。

Conclusion: 熵门控能够检测高熵源崩溃（而非自信错误的低熵源），实现了源模型退化时的优雅衰减，验证了基于熵的可信门控机制的有效性。

Abstract: Continual test-time adaptation (CTTA) updates a pretrained model online on an unlabeled, non-stationary stream while anchoring it to a frozen source checkpoint. This anchor is useful only when the source remains reliable. On CCC-Hard, however, a ResNet-50 source falls to approximately $1.3\%$ top-$1$ accuracy, while existing source-anchored CTTA methods continue applying the same anchor strength. We call this failure mode blind anchoring and propose RMemSafe, a reliability-gated extension of ROID that uses the frozen source's normalized predictive entropy to attenuate all explicit source-coupled uses in the objective. When the source posterior approaches uniformity, the gate closes: the source anchor and agreement filter vanish, and the objective reduces to a source-agnostic fallback comprising ROID's base losses plus marginal calibration. Combined with ASR, RMemSafe achieves the lowest error on $8$ of $9$ matched-split continual-corruption cells and is the best reset-based method on all $9$, improving ROID+ASR by $1.05$~pp on ResNet-50 and $0.48$~pp on ViT-B/16. A controlled source-degradation sweep shows a $1.13{\times}$ shallower harm slope than ROID+ASR, consistent with the graceful-decay prediction. The entropy gate detects high-entropy source collapse, not confidently wrong low-entropy sources; this scope is explicitly evaluated and discussed.

</details>


### [235] [SurF: A Generative Model for Multivariate Irregular Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14069)
*Mohammad R. Rezaei,Tejas Balaji,Rahul G. Krishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SurF利用时间重标定理将不规则事件流转换为i.i.d.指数噪声，通过可学习的累积强度参数化和Transformer编码器，在六个真实基准上实现了最优或接近最优的时间RMSE，迈出了异步事件流基础模型的第一步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不规则采样的多元事件流在生成建模中面临两个核心困难：基于token化的方法无法处理跨数量级的间隔变化，而神经时间点过程受限于窗口级数值积分。现有方法缺乏能够跨异构数据集训练的统一框架。

Method: (i) 利用时间重标定理作为事件序列与i.i.d.单位速率指数噪声之间的可学习双射；(ii) 提出三种高效的累积强度参数化方案以扩展到长序列；(iii) 采用基于Transformer的编码器实现多数据集预训练。

Result: 在六个真实世界基准中，SurF在Earthquake、Retweet和Taobao上达到了最优时间RMSE；在严格留一协议下，保留的检查点在5/6数据集中击败了所有经典和神经自回归基线，在Amazon和Earthquake上击败了所有基线。

Conclusion: SurF展示了跨异构事件流数据集训练单一生成模型的可行性，是迈向异步事件流基础模型的重要初始步骤。

Abstract: Irregularly sampled multivariate event streams remain a stubbornly difficult modality for generative modeling: tokenization-based approaches break down when inter-event intervals vary by orders of magnitude, and neural temporal point processes are bottlenecked by window-level numerical quadrature. We (i) propose SurF, a generative model that uses the Time Rescaling Theorem (TRT) as a learnable bijection between event sequences and i.i.d.\ unit-rate exponential noise, enabling a single model to be trained across heterogeneous event-stream datasets; (ii) three efficient parameterizations of the cumulative intensity that scale to long sequences; and (iii) a Transformer-based encoder for multi-dataset pretraining. On six real-world benchmarks, SurF achieves the best reported time RMSE on Earthquake, Retweet, and Taobao, and is within trial-level noise of the strongest specialist on the remaining three. Under a strict leave-one-out protocol, the held-out checkpoint beats every classical and neural-autoregressive baseline on 5/6 datasets and beats every baseline on Amazon and Earthquake, an initial step toward foundation models over asynchronous event streams.

</details>


### [236] [Fair and Calibrated Toxicity Detection with Robust Training and Abstention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14074)
*Mokshit Surana*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文论证毒性分类的公平性需同时评估排序、校准和弃权三个维度，发现训练时干预和后处理机制不可独立评估。实验表明：校准差异是隐藏的公平违规；训练干预只是重塑而非消除差异；后处理方法继承训练失败模式；弃权本身也不公平。主张SRAI公平需多轴框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有毒性分类公平性研究通常只关注单一维度（如排序公平性或校准），忽略了排序、校准和弃权三个维度的内在关联。同时，训练时干预与后处理安全机制常被独立评估，但前者决定了后者的有效性。本文旨在建立一个多轴公平性评估框架，揭示单一维度评估带来的误导性结论。

Method: 对比三种训练方法（ERM、实例级重加权、Group DRO）在三个公平性维度（排序、校准、弃权）上的表现，并结合三种后处理机制（温度缩放、基于置信度的弃权、逐身份阈值优化）。评估指标包括子组AUC、BPSN/BNSP AUC、误差差距和子组期望校准误差（ECE），使用bootstrap置信区间（n=1000）。

Result: （1）ERM聚合校准近乎完美（0.013），但所有身份子组均显著校准偏差（+0.029至+0.134）。（2）重加权ERM改善排序（BPSN AUC +0.06至+0.12）但校准公平差距恶化最多+0.232；Group DRO消除校准差异但全局校准恶化（ECE 0.118）。（3）温度缩放因校准非均匀性而失效；置信度弃权在ERM下有效但DRO下失效。（4）置信度弃权对背景内容比对身份提及内容帮助更大。

Conclusion: 毒性分类的公平性不能仅依赖聚合指标（如总体AUC或ECE），需同时评估排序、校准和弃权三个维度。仅在一个维度上表现良好的方法可能在另一个维度上造成隐藏的公平危害。SRAI公平性评估应采用多轴框架，因为仅在聚合排序上不同的方法，在决定实际危害的失败模式上可能有显著差异。

Abstract: Fairness in toxicity classification involves three integrated axes: ranking, calibration, and abstention. Training-time interventions and post-hoc safety mechanisms cannot be evaluated independently because the former determines the efficacy of the latter. We compare Empirical Risk Minimization (ERM), instance-level reweighting, and Group DRO across these axes, combined with temperature scaling, confidence-based abstention, and per-identity threshold optimization. Evaluation uses subgroup AUC, BPSN/BNSP AUC, error gaps, and per-subgroup Expected Calibration Error (ECE) with bootstrap CIs ($n = 1000$).
  We report four findings. (1) Calibration disparity is a hidden fairness violation. ERM has near-perfect aggregate calibration ($0.013$) but is significantly miscalibrated across all identity subgroups ($+0.029$ to $+0.134$). (2) Training interventions reshape rather than eliminate disparity. Reweighted ERM improves ranking (BPSN AUC $+0.06$ to $+0.12$) but worsens the calibration-fairness gap by up to $+0.232$. Group DRO eliminates calibration disparity but only by becoming uniformly miscalibrated globally (ECE $0.118$). (3) Post-hoc methods inherit training failure modes. Temperature scaling fails because miscalibration is non-uniform. Confidence-based abstention works under ERM but breaks under DRO, where the risk-coverage curve rises with deferral. (4) Abstention itself is unfair. Confidence-based deferral helps background content far more than identity-mentioning content. We argue that SRAI fairness requires a multi-axis framework: methods that differ only in aggregate ranking can differ sharply in failure modes that determine real-world harm.

</details>


### [237] [Towards Fine-Grained and Verifiable Concept Bottleneck Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14210)
*Yingying Fang,Haijie Xu,Shuang Wu,Mariathasan Anish,Guang Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种细粒度概念瓶颈模型框架，将每个概念锚定在局部视觉证据上，使概念表示既透明又可验证，在医学影像基准上达到与标准CBM相当的预测性能，同时显著提升了可解释性和可信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概念瓶颈模型（CBM）虽然通过引入人类可理解的概念提升了可解释性，但无法验证预测的概念是否对应正确的视觉证据，容易学习虚假相关性，限制了其可靠性。

Method: 提出细粒度CBM框架，将每个概念锚定在局部化视觉证据上，使概念编码的位置和方式可被直接检查。

Result: 在医学影像基准上，学习到的概念空间信息完整，预测性能与标准CBM相当，同时显著提升了透明度；不同于事后归因方法，该方法同时验证了概念表示的存在性和正确性。

Conclusion: 该方法弥合了可解释性与可验证性之间的差距，增强了CBM的信任度，为更可靠、临床可操作的概念学习系统奠定了基础。

Abstract: Concept Bottleneck Models (CBMs) offer interpretable alternatives to black-box predictors by introducing human-relatable concepts before the final output. However, existing CBMs struggle to verify whether predicted concepts correspond to the correct visual evidence, limiting their reliability. We propose a fine-grained CBM framework that grounds each concept in localized visual evidence, enabling direct inspection of where and how concepts are encoded. This design allows users to interpret predictions and verify that the model learns intended concepts rather than spurious correlations. Experiments on medical imaging benchmarks show that our learned concept space is information-complete and achieves predictive performance comparable to standard CBMs, while substantially improving transparency. Unlike post-hoc attribution methods, our framework validates both the presence and correctness of concept representations, bridging interpretability with verifiability. Our approach enhances the trustworthiness of CBMs and establishes a principled mechanism for human-model interaction at the concept level, paving the way toward more reliable and clinically actionable concept-based learning systems.

</details>


### [238] [PreFT: Prefill-only finetuning for efficient inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14217)
*Andrew Lanpouthakoun,Aryaman Arora,Zhengxuan Wu,Dhruv Pai,Ben Keigwin,Dan Jurafsky,Christopher Potts*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PreFT（仅预填充微调），在推理时仅对预填充（prefill）阶段应用适配器，解码（decode）阶段丢弃，以解决多适配器服务中传统PEFT因预填充与解码吞吐量不匹配导致的低效问题。实验表明，PreFT在Llama 3.1 70B上服务512个适配器时吞吐量提升1.9倍，虽在SFT任务上评估损失略高但可通过增加秩补偿，在RL任务上接近标准PEFT性能，实现了更好的精度-吞吐量权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PEFT（如LoRA）在服务多用户个性化模型时，预填充阶段（批量处理大量token）和解码阶段（自回归逐个生成token）之间存在严重的吞吐量不匹配，导致解码阶段成为瓶颈，即使使用专用内核和内存管理技术也无法有效提升多适配器服务吞吐量。

Method: 提出PreFT（Prefill-only Finetuning），核心思想是仅在预填充阶段对输入token应用适配器（如LoRA、ReFT），在解码生成阶段丢弃适配器。在vLLM推理引擎上实现了两种预填充仅PEFT方法（LoRA和ReFT），并开发了高效实现。

Result: 1) 服务效率：在Llama 3.1 70B上服务512个适配器时，PreFT吞吐量是传统PEFT的1.9倍。2) SFT任务：PreFT评估损失高于PEFT，但可通过增加秩（rank）补偿且几乎不损失吞吐量。3) RL任务：PreFT性能与标准PEFT接近。

Conclusion: PreFT验证了仅预填充LLM适配是一种比现有PEFT更有利的精度-吞吐量权衡方案，适用于个性化模型服务场景。

Abstract: Large language models can now be personalised efficiently at scale using parameter efficient finetuning methods (PEFTs), but serving user-specific PEFTs harms throughput, even with specialised kernels and memory management techniques. This is because, theoretically and empirically, a mismatch exists between prefill (processing a large number of tokens at once) and decode (generating a single token autoregressively): the latter has far lower throughput when serving multiple adapters. Rather than optimising performance relative to parameter count, for efficient multi-adapter serving, we instead ought to optimise performance relative to serving throughput. We therefore propose PreFT (Prefill-only Finetuning), wherein we only apply the adapter to prefill tokens and discard it afterwards. PreFT significantly increases throughput with minimal effect on performance. We develop and release an efficient implementation of two prefill-only PEFTs, LoRA and ReFT, on the vLLM inference engine. We first show that serving multi-user PreFTs is more efficient than traditional PEFTs ($1.9\times$ the throughput when serving $512$ adapters on Llama 3.1 70B). Then, we compare the performance of prefill-only vs. all-token adapters on a variety of supervised finetuning and reinforcement learning tasks with LMs at varying scales. On SFT, we observe that the evaluation loss of PreFTs is higher than PEFTs, but can be compensated by increasing rank with nearly no reduction in throughput. On RL, we consistently find that PreFTs approach parity with standard PEFTs. Together, this work validates prefill-only adaptation of LLMs as a more favourable accuracy-throughput tradeoff than existing PEFTs for personalised serving.

</details>


### [239] [Diagnosing Training Inference Mismatch in LLM Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14220)
*Tianle Zhong,Neiwen Ling,Yifan Pi,Zijun Wei,Tianshu Yu,Geoffrey Fox,Peng Wu,Xiao Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 现代LLM强化学习系统中， rollout生成与策略优化两阶段因实现差异导致训练-推理不匹配（TIM），即使相同模型权重下也会对同一序列赋予不同概率值。本文通过零不匹配诊断环境（VeXact）隔离TIM，证明微小的词级数值分歧可独立导致训练崩溃，并揭示TIM会改变有效优化问题，提出缓解措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM RL系统中rollout生成和策略优化两个阶段本应产生完全匹配的token概率，但实现差异导致两者分配不同值，产生训练-推理不匹配（TIM）。由于TIM与离策略漂移及常见稳定机制纠缠在一起，难以被检查和诊断，因此需要系统性地隔离和研究TIM的影响。

Method: 设计一个零不匹配诊断环境（VeXact）来隔离TIM，排除其他混杂因素；在受控条件下观察小规模词级数值分歧对训练稳定性的独立影响；分析TIM如何改变有效优化问题；识别并验证一系列缓解措施。

Result: 微小的词级数值分歧可以独立地导致训练崩溃；TIM会改变有效的优化目标函数；找到了一组能够缓解TIM的补救措施。

Conclusion: TIM并非无害的数值噪声，而是一种系统级别的扰动，在分析LLM RL稳定性时应将其视为一阶因素加以处理。

Abstract: Modern LLM RL systems separate rollout generation from policy optimization. These two stages are expected to produce token probabilities that match exactly. However, implementation differences can make them assign different values to the same sequence under the same model weights, inducing Training-Inference Mismatch (TIM). TIM is difficult to inspect because it is entangled with off-policy drift and common stabilization mechanisms. In this work, we isolate TIM in a zero-mismatch diagnostic setting (VeXact), and show that small token-level numerical disagreements can independently cause training collapse. We further show that TIM changes the effective optimization problem, and identify a set of remedies that could mitigate TIM. Our results suggest that TIM is not benign numerical noise, but a systems-level perturbation that should be treated as a first-order factor in analyzing LLM RL stability.

</details>


### [240] [AudioMosaic: Contrastive Masked Audio Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14231)
*Hanxun Huang,Qizhou Wang,Xingjun Ma,Cihang Xie,Christopher Leckie,Sarah Erfani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AudioMosaic是一种基于对比学习的音频自监督学习编码器，通过结构化时频掩码构建正样本对，支持大批量高效训练，在多个音频基准上取得SOTA性能，并能提升音频-语言模型的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相比生成式重构目标，对比学习方法在音频SSL中探索较少，主要受限于音频数据增强设计的困难以及对比预训练所需的大批量大小。作者旨在开发一种高效的对比学习音频编码器，以学习更具判别力的通用音频表征。

Method: 在预训练阶段，对声谱图patches应用结构化的时频掩码来构造正样本对，从而降低内存使用并支持高效的大批量训练。

Result: 在多个标准音频基准上，在线性探测和微调设置下均达到SOTA性能；将预训练的AudioMosaic编码器集成到音频-语言模型中，进一步提升了音频-语言任务的表现。

Conclusion: 对比学习方法通过精心设计的正样本构造策略，能够超越生成式方法，学习到更具判别力的音频表征，且具有良好的跨数据集、跨领域和跨声学条件的迁移能力。

Abstract: Audio self-supervised learning (SSL) aims to learn general-purpose representations from large-scale unlabeled audio data. While recent advances have been driven mainly by generative reconstruction objectives, contrastive approaches remain less explored, partly due to the difficulty of designing effective audio augmentations and the large batch sizes required for contrastive pre-training. We introduce \textbf{AudioMosaic}, a contrastive learning-based audio encoder for general audio understanding. During pre-training, AudioMosaic constructs positive pairs by applying structured time-frequency masking to spectrogram patches, which reduces memory usage and enables efficient large-batch training. Compared with generative approaches, the AudioMosaic encoder learns more discriminative utterance-level representations that demonstrate strong transferability across datasets, domains, and acoustic conditions. Extensive experiments show that AudioMosaic achieves state-of-the-art performance on several standard audio benchmarks under both linear probing and fine-tuning. We further show that integrating the pretrained AudioMosaic encoder into audio-language models improves performance on audio-language tasks. The code is publicly available in our \href{https://github.com/HanxunH/AudioMosaic}{GitHub repository}.

</details>


### [241] [Quantum Advantage in Multi Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14235)
*Simranjeet Singh Dahia,Claudia Szabo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文实证评估了量子纠缠在多智能体强化学习（QMARL）中的协调作用。在CHSH博弈中，纠缠QMARL智能体达到0.854胜率（超越经典上限0.75），证明量子优势源于纠缠而非量子电路本身；在CoopNav任务中，无纠缠QMARL成功率达~0.85，远超经典MAA2C的~0.40，且混合架构（量子Actor+经典集中式Critic）表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有QMARL研究缺乏可证明的基线对照，无法严格区分量子优势与算法偶然性。本文旨在通过具有理论边界的基准任务（CHSH博弈）和严谨实验设计，明确量子纠缠在智能体协调中的实际作用。

Method: 采用去中心化QMARL框架，使用变分量子电路（VQC）作为Actor，共享纠缠态。在CHSH博弈（理论经典上限0.75）与CoopNav任务中，对比纠缠/非纠缠量子电路、经典MAA2C基线及混合架构（量子Actor+经典Critic）的性能。

Result: （1）CHSH博弈：纠缠QMARL达到0.854（逼近Tsirelson极限），无纠缠量子电路仅0.75（匹配经典基线）；（2）纠缠结构影响显著：部分Bell态促进协调，部分反而损害性能；（3）CoopNav：无纠缠QMARL成功率~0.85（vs 经典~0.40），混合架构最优。

Conclusion: 量子纠缠是QMARL实现量子优势的核心协调机制，而非量子电路本身。混合量子-经典架构（量子Actor+经典Critic）在复杂任务中表现最佳，为未来QMARL设计提供方向。

Abstract: We present an empirical evaluation of quantum entanglement in agent coordination within quantum multi agent reinforcement learning (QMARL). While QMARL has attracted growing interest recently, most prior work evaluates quantum policies without provable baselines, making it impossible to rigorously distinguish quantum advantage from algorithmic coincidence. We address this directly by evaluating a decentralized QMARL framework with variational quantum circuit (VQC) actors with shared entangled states. In the CHSH game, which has a mathematically proven classical performance ceiling of 0.75 win rate, we show that entangled QMARL agents approach the Tsirelson limit of 0.854, providing clear evidence of their quantum advantage. We show that unentangled quantum circuits match the classical baseline, confirming that entanglement and not the quantum circuit itself is the active coordination mechanism. We also explore the effect of specific entanglement structures, as some Bell states enable coordination gains while others actively harm performance. On cooperative navigation (CoopNav), QMARL without entanglement achieves $\sim2\times$ improvement in success rate over classical MAA2C ($\sim$0.85 versus $\sim$0.40), with the hybrid configuration, quantum actor paired with a classical centralised critic, outperforming both fully classical and fully quantum solutions. We present our experimental analysis and discuss future work.

</details>


### [242] [Active Learners as Efficient PRP Rerankers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14236)
*Jeremías Figueiredo Paschmann,Juan Kaplan,Francisco Nattero Santiago Mauricio Barron Bucolo,Juan Wisznia,Luciano del Corro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将PRP重排序重新定义为基于噪声成对比较的主动学习，使用随机方向查询消除位置偏差，在调用受限条件下显著提升NDCG@10效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PRP方法依赖排序算法恢复全排列，但LLM的成对判断存在噪声、顺序敏感性和不可传递性，导致排序假设不成立；且在调用预算受限时，截断排序无法可靠获得top-K结果。

Method: 将PRP重排序重新框架为从噪声成对比较中进行主动学习；提出随机方向查询（randomized-direction oracle），每次仅用单次LLM调用，将系统性的位置偏差转化为零均值噪声，实现无偏聚合排序。

Result: 主动排序器可作为PRP的即插即用替代方案，在调用受限条件下显著提升NDCG@10性能。

Conclusion: 主动学习框架和随机方向查询策略有效解决了PRP中噪声和位置偏差问题，在有限调用预算下实现了更可靠的重排序性能。

Abstract: Pairwise Ranking Prompting (PRP) elicits pairwise preference judgments from an LLM, which are then aggregated into a ranking, usually via classical sorting algorithms. However, judgments are noisy, order-sensitive, and sometimes intransitive, so sorting assumptions do not match the setting. Because sorting aims to recover a full permutation, truncating it to meet a call budget does not produce a dependable top-K. We thus reframe PRP reranking as active learning from noisy pairwise comparisons and show that active rankers are drop-in replacements that improve NDCG@10 per call in the call-constrained regime. Our noise-robust framework also introduces a randomized-direction oracle that uses a single LLM call per pair. This approach converts systematic position bias into zero-mean noise, enabling unbiased aggregate ranking without the cost of bidirectional calls.

</details>


### [243] [Paraphrasing Attack Resilience of Various AI-Generated Text Detection Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14240)
*Andrii Shportko,Inessa Verbitsky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了三种机器生成文本检测方法（微调RoBERTa、Binoculars和文本特征分析）及其集成模型在面对释义攻击时的鲁棒性，发现包含Binoculars的集成方法性能最强但攻击下性能损失也最严重，揭示了AI文本检测中性能与鲁棒性之间的权衡困境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的广泛使用带来了抄袭和虚假信息传播等问题，同时AI检测绕过工具也在兴起，因此迫切需要可靠的机器生成文本检测方法。现有研究缺乏对检测方法在面对攻击时鲁棒性的系统评估。

Method: 评估了三种检测方法：微调RoBERTa、Binoculars和文本特征分析，并使用随机森林分类器构建它们的集成模型，通过释义攻击测试各方法的鲁棒性。

Result: 包含Binoculars的集成方法在正常情况下检测性能最强，但在面对释义攻击时性能下降最为显著，揭示了性能与鲁棒性之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 当前最先进的AI文本检测技术在性能与鲁棒性之间存在矛盾，这挑战了现有技术可靠性的普遍认知。

Abstract: The recent large-scale emergence of LLMs has left an open space for dealing with their consequences, such as plagiarism or the spread of false information on the Internet. Coupling this with the rise of AI detector bypassing tools, reliable machine-generated text detection is in increasingly high demand. We investigate the paraphrasing attack resilience of various machine-generated text detection methods, evaluating three approaches: fine-tuned RoBERTa, Binoculars, and text feature analysis, along with their ensembles using Random Forest classifiers. We discovered that Binoculars-inclusive ensembles yield the strongest results, but they also suffer the most significant losses during attacks. In this paper, we present the dichotomy of performance versus resilience in the world of AI text detection, which complicates the current perception of reliability among state-of-the-art techniques.

</details>


### [244] [Artificial Intelligence-Assistant Cardiotocography: Unified Model for Signal Reconstruction, Fetal Heart Rate Analysis, and Variability Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14242)
*Xiaohua Wang,Kai Yu,XuXiao Liang,Liang Wang,Chao Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于AI的FHrCTG模型，用于胎心率监测，通过大规模预训练（558,412个未标记数据）和专家标注微调（7,266条数据）实现噪声抑制和信号重建。引入IOL方法将率分析转化为分类判断，在检测减速和加速方面分别达到89.13%/87.78%和62.5%/92.04%的敏感度/特异度，在Fischer评分标准下AUC达到0.7214（周期性）和0.9643（振幅变化）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统胎心率监测方法受限于设备性能、数据传输噪声和医生主观判断，亟需一种客观、精准且抗干扰的自动化分析方法来预防胎儿窘迫和不良妊娠结局。

Method: 开发了专门的AI-based FHrCTG模型，先使用558,412个未标记数据进行预训练，再通过7,266条专家审核数据微调；提出Intersection Overlapping Labels (IOL)方法将率分析转化为分类判断，实现信号重建和噪声抑制。

Result: 检测关键胎心率减速的敏感度89.13%、特异度87.78%；加速敏感度62.5%、特异度92.04%。基于Fischer标准，周期性验证AUC为0.7214，振幅变化验证AUC为0.9643。

Conclusion: 该AI模型在胎心率监测中表现出高敏感度和特异度，能有效克服传统方法局限，具有临床应用的潜力。

Abstract: The monitoring of fetal heart rate (FHR) and the assessment of its variability are crucial for preventing fetal compromise and adverse outcomes. However, traditional methods encounter limitations arising from equipment performance, data transmission, and subjective assessments by doctors. We have developed a tailored AI-based FHrCTG model specifically for FHR monitoring, which effectively mitigates noise interference and precisely reconstructs signals. Our model was pre-trained on a massive dataset consisting of 558,412 unlabeled data points and further refined using 7,266 expert-reviewed entries. To validate FHR, we introduced the Intersection Overlapping Labels (IOL) approach, which transforms rate analysis into categorical judgments. Testing revealed that our model demonstrates high sensitivity and specificity in detecting critical FHR decelerations (89.13% and 87.78%, respectively) and accelerations (62.5% and 92.04%, respectively). Furthermore, based on Fischer's criteria for clinical application, our model achieved impressive AUC scores of 0.7214 and 0.9643 for verifying FHR periodicity and amplitude variation, respectively.

</details>


### [245] [Architecture-Aware Explanation Auditing for Industrial Visual Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14255)
*Sibo Jia,Zihang Zhao,Kunrong Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于架构感知的说明审计协议，发现解释方法的忠实度受其与模型原生决策机制的结构距离限制。在WM-811K晶圆图上，ViT-Tiny+Attention Rollout取得了最优的Deletion AUC（0.211），但分类精度较低；Swin-Tiny虽为Transformer但因层次化特征图结构与Grad-CAM兼容。模型无关方法RISE压缩所有模型至约0.1的Deletion AUC，表明原生读取是兼容性原则而非最优性保证。评审结果依赖（模型、解释器、扰动算子）三元组，需要结合任务和数据特征进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工业视觉检测系统中的深度分类器热力图解释往往视觉上看似合理，却未能真正反映驱动模型决策的图像区域，缺乏评估解释方法忠实度的系统协议。

Method: 基于原生读取假说（native-readout hypothesis），设计架构感知的解释审计协议，通过扰动实验（零填充、模糊填充）测量解释方法的忠实度（Deletion AUC），并在WM-811K和MVTec AD数据集上比较不同架构（ViT、Swin、ResNet等）与解释方法（Attention Rollout、Grad-CAM、RISE）的组合。

Result: ViT-Tiny+Attention Rollout在零填充协议下Deletion AUC为0.211，优于Swin-Tiny/ResNet18+CBAM/DenseNet121+Grad-CAM（0.432-0.525），效果量abs(Cohen's d)>1.1；RISE模型无关方法将所有模型的Deletion AUC压缩至约0.1；模糊填充下家族排序反转。

Conclusion: 解释路径应与模型架构基于读取结构协同设计，部署热力图应附带定量忠实度指标；解释忠实度排名是（模型、解释器、扰动算子）三元组的联合属性，具有任务和数据集依赖性。

Abstract: Industrial visual inspection systems increasingly rely on deep classifiers whose heatmap explanations may appear visually plausible while failing to identify the image regions that actually drive model decisions. This paper operationalizes an architecture-aware explanation audit protocol grounded in the native-readout hypothesis: the perturbation-based faithfulness of an explanation method is bounded by its structural distance from the model's native decision mechanism. On WM-811K wafer maps (9 classes, 172k images) under a three-seed zero-fill perturbation protocol, ViT-Tiny + Attention Rollout attains Deletion AUC 0.211 against 0.432-0.525 for Swin-Tiny / ResNet18+CBAM / DenseNet121 + Grad-CAM (abs(Cohen's d) > 1.1), despite lower classification accuracy. Swin-Tiny disentangles architecture family from readout structure: despite being a Transformer, its spatial feature-map hierarchy makes it Grad-CAM compatible, showing that the operative factor is readout structure rather than architecture family. A model-agnostic control (RISE) compresses all families to Deletion AUC about 0.1, indicating the gap arises from the explainer pathway; notably, RISE outperforms all native methods, so native readout is a compatibility principle rather than an optimality guarantee. A blur-fill sensitivity analysis shows that the family ordering reverses under a different perturbation baseline, reinforcing that faithfulness rankings are joint properties of (model, explainer, perturbation operator) triples. An exploratory boundary-condition study on MVTec AD (pretrained models) indicates that audit results are dataset/task dependent and identifies conditions requiring qualification. The protocol yields actionable guidance: explanation pathways should be co-designed with model architectures based on readout structure, and deployed heatmaps should be accompanied by quantitative faithfulness metrics.

</details>


### [246] [Dynamics of the Transformer Residual Stream: Coupling Spectral Geometry to Network Topology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14258)
*Jesseba Fernando,Grigori Guitchounts*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Large language models are remarkably capable, yet how computation propagates through their layers remains poorly understood. A growing line of work treats depth as discrete time and the residual stream as a dynamical system, where each layer's nonlinear update has a local linear description. However, previous analyses have relied on scalar summaries or approximate linearizations, leaving the full spectral geometry of trained LLMs unknown. We perform full Jacobian eigendecomposition across three production--scale LLMs and show that training installs a monotonic spectral gradient through depth -- from non-normal, rotation-dominated early layers to near--symmetric late layers -- together with a cumulative low-rank bottleneck that funnels perturbations into a small fraction of the residual stream's effective dimensions. Our experiments reveal that this gradient and the dimensional collapse are learned rather than architectural, and is largely dissolved when structured non-normality is removed. We further show that the topological positioning of graph communities predicts whether the Jacobian amplifies or suppresses them, with the sign of the coupling determined by the local operator type, a relationship absent at initialization. These results map a learned spectral geometry in LLMs that links perturbation propagation and compression to the network's functional topology.

</details>


### [247] [Finite Sample Bounds for Learning with Score Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14168)
*Devin Smedira,Abhijith Jayakumar,Sidhant Misra,Marc Vuffray,Andrey Y. Lokhov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次为非渐近条件下的多项式指数族分布结构学习提供了样本复杂度分析，证明了得分匹配方法所需的样本量随模型维度呈多项式增长，克服了以往仅能给出渐近界的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 得分匹配在高维连续指数族分布学习中因计算效率高而被广泛使用，但其统计性质的理论理解仍然不足，现有工作仅能提供渐近样本复杂度界，缺乏非渐近分析。

Method: 对多项式指数族分布采用得分匹配方法进行结构学习，推导出非渐近的样本复杂度界，证明样本量随模型维度呈多项式依赖关系。

Result: 导出了首个非渐近样本复杂度界，显示样本复杂度与模型维度之间存在多项式关系。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了得分匹配非渐近理论分析的空白，为高维统计中连续指数族分布的学习提供了理论保障。

Abstract: Learning of continuous exponential family distributions with unbounded support remains an important area of research for both theory and applications in high-dimensional statistics. In recent years, score matching has become a widely used method for learning exponential families with continuous variables due to its computational ease when compared against maximum likelihood estimation. However, theoretical understanding of the statistical properties of score matching is still lacking. In this work, we provide a non-asymptotic sample complexity analysis for learning the structure of exponential families of polynomials with score matching. The derived sample bounds show a polynomial dependence on the model dimension. These bounds are the first of its kind, as all prior work has shown only asymptotic bounds on the sample complexity.

</details>


### [248] [Smooth Multi-Policy Causal Effect Estimation in Longitudinal Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14284)
*Wenxin Chen,Weishen Pan,Kyra Gan,Fei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种策略感知的迭代条件期望(ICE)Q函数重参数化方法，通过共享策略编码器实现多个动态治疗策略的联合估计。该编码器使用核均值嵌入训练，确保表征空间反映总体策略差异。经过LTMLE校正后，设计对二阶余项施加结构性约束以稳定有限样本方差。实验表明PEQ-Net优于现有ICE方法，尤其在评估相近策略时显著降低均方根误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统纵向因果推断方法孤立估计每个动态治疗策略，导致反事实之间无法共享信息，这种分离估计范式会在标准LTMLE去偏后仍引入结构性未控制的二阶偏差，膨胀有限样本方差。

Method: 提出策略感知的ICE Q函数重参数化，设计PEQ-Net架构：核心是共享策略编码器，通过核均值嵌入训练学习表征空间；随后应用LTMLE校正步骤，对二阶余项施加结构性约束。

Result: 在半合成数据集上，PEQ-Net一致优于现有ICE方法，尤其在评估紧密相关策略时，均方根误差大幅降低。

Conclusion: 联合估计策略表征能有效控制二阶偏差、稳定有限样本方差，为多动态治疗策略的因果推断提供了更高效的方法。

Abstract: Comparative evaluation of multiple dynamic treatment policies is essential for healthcare and policy decisions, yet conventional longitudinal causal inference methods estimate each in isolation, preventing information sharing across counterfactuals. We demonstrate that this separate estimation paradigm induces a structurally uncontrolled second-order bias, inflating finite-sample variance even after standard debiasing with longitudinal targeted maximum likelihood estimation(LTMLE). To address this, we propose a policy-aware reparameterization of Iterative Conditional Expectation (ICE) Q-functions that enables joint estimation through shared representations. We implement this approach in the Policy-Encoded Q Network (PEQ-Net), an architecture centered on a shared policy encoder. The encoder is trained using kernel mean embeddings, ensuring that the learned representation space reflects population-level policy dissimilarities. After applying an LTMLE correction step, we prove this design imposes a structural constraint on the second-order remainder, thereby stabilizing finite-sample variance. Experiments on semi-synthetic datasets demonstrate that PEQ-Net consistently outperforms existing ICE-based methods, achieving substantial reductions in root-mean-square error, particularly when evaluating closely related policies.

</details>


### [249] [Minimal-Intervention KV Retention: A Design-Space Study and a Diversity-Penalty Survivor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14292)
*Libo Sun,Po-wei Harn,Peixiong He,Xiao Qin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在低预算KV缓存压缩的拥挤设计空间中，研究了五种机制家族下的七种方案均被拒绝。提出α方法，将TriAttention的argmax-top-k替换为受设施选址启发的贪婪选择，加入V空间冗余惩罚。在预注册协议下，α在部分模型/预算组合上通过了Bonferroni校正，表明最小的评分修改比重大结构重设计更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低预算下KV缓存压缩的设计空间拥挤，现有七个机制（涵盖缓存表示、逐头路由、压缩节奏、解码行为、预算内评分五个家族）在匹配均值缓存设置下均被拒绝，亟需更有效的压缩方法。

Method: 提出α方法，对TriAttention的保留评分器进行单函数修改：将argmax-top-k替换为受设施选址启发的贪婪选择，并引入V空间冗余惩罚（权重λ）。采用预注册协议：在冻结的开发集上调优λ，在不相交的保留集上确认结果。

Result: 在λ=0.5时，α在四个（模型，预算）单元中的两个（Qwen b=128和Llama b=64）通过了Bonferroni校正，没有单元出现显著负向结果，触发了预注册的Branch A。

Conclusion: 最小的评分修改（α）在低预算KV缓存压缩中比更重的结构重设计更有效；匹配内存、sympy评分、保留集确认的协议标准使得这种不对称性得以显现。

Abstract: KV-cache compression at small budgets is a crowded design space spanning cache representation, head-wise routing, compression cadence, decoding behavior, and within-budget scoring.
